# Kazakhstan — Maths books

**KAZAKHSTAN is CLOSED in the library, and its maths set is
the largest of any closed country: 203 books recorded, and
all 203 are in hand** (the library record calls 198 of them
student books and 5 teacher books; the books themselves
correct that split in places — see the corrections below).
All 203 files were read and filed below in six grade bands
on 2026-11-04: **30,174 PDF pages**, resolving into **87
distinct works**. Every band's filing then passed a separate
citation check (checker ≠ reader): **1,555 anchors checked
against the books' own pages — 1,416 passed, 132 corrected
in place, 7 not found and removed or replaced**. The
per-band counts and every correction stand in the check
block at the end of each band below.

**The file count, reconciled plainly:** progress notes
during the read spoke of 210 files. That number was wrong:
it added Band C's 24 files a second time to a partial sum
that already contained them. The true count is **203**,
verified every way it can be counted — 203 rows in the
library's set table, 203 PDFs grabbed and held, and the six
band lists (44 + 62 + 24 + 32 + 24 + 17) summing to 203
unique files with no file in two bands.

Kazakhstan does not run one national maths course: the
ministry's approved list carries competing commercial series
side by side, and the record holds them in parallel —
Almatykitap (Akpaeva), Atamura (Aldamuratova, Ospanov,
Shynybekov), Mektep (Abylkasymova, Smirnov), Keleshek-2030
(Soltan), the NIS schools' own integrated *Mathematics*
course, and an English-language line from Astana-kitap —
most titles in two parts per grade and in Kazakh and Russian
editions, with Uyghur and Uzbek minority-language editions
for several Mektep and Atamura titles. Grades 1–9 are held
richly and in depth. The top of the set is thin:

**THE GAP, PROVEN FROM THE BOOKS: the in-hand set contains
no Grade 10 Algebra of any kind, and no Grade 11 or Grade 12
mathematics of any kind.** Grade 10 is present only as
geometry, and only indirectly. File 573 is the
**humanities-track** Smirnov & Tuyakov *Geometry 10*
(Mektep, 2019, ISBN 978-601-07-1147-1) — its contents and
index contain no surface areas, no volumes, no sections of
polyhedra and no bodies of revolution. File 737, recorded
as a Grade 10 textbook, is by its own preface a
**worked-solutions manual** for the Soltan et al.
*Geometry 10* textbook (natural-mathematics track) — a
textbook that is itself **not in hand** — and the manual's
ISBN field is printed blank in the book. So the in-hand
Kazakhstan maths set effectively tops out at Grade 9
(complete: three algebra and three geometry textbooks, each
in Kazakh + Russian, plus an English-language algebra),
Grade 10 is represented only partially and indirectly, and
Grades 11–12 are entirely absent. This is a library-side
gap, recorded here, not something this read can fix.

**What the books prove about the record's student/teacher
split:** the five files recorded as teacher books are
genuine (the two Zhakupova Grade 2 guides in Band A, the two
Grade 4 teacher books in Band B, and file 1557 in Band C).
But eight further files recorded as student textbooks are
teacher or companion material by their own title pages:
file 1558 (the Kazakh edition of the electronic teacher
supplement to Mektep Grade 6 — the same work whose Russian
edition is the recorded teacher file 1557), files 1555/1556
(the teacher's electronic supplement to Mektep Algebra 8),
files 1639/1641 (didactic-materials books) and 1640/1642
(problem collections) for the Mektep Grade 7 textbooks, and
file 737 (the Grade 10 solutions manual above). None of the
companions contains student theory of its own; each is filed
below as a component of the textbook it serves.

**Read method:** every file's cover, title page and imprint
were inspected, and covers/imprints govern over the record
throughout. Books with a usable text layer were read whole
through it; the many image-only scans (their only text layer
is the OKULYK watermark) were read from rendered pages —
each band's method note states honestly which books were
read page by page and which from their full printed contents
plus rendered spreads of every section. All filings are
Muse's own summaries and structural notes, with only very
short quoted fragments.

**Held-copy defects (content covered by sister editions
unless stated):** Band B file 858 (Grade 3 Russian Part 3,
2019 printing) lacks printed pp. 3–4 and its back cover —
the same pages are held in the 2023 reprint, file 1223.
Band D file 1373 (Uyghur Algebra 7) ends at printed p. 205
of 272, and file 1424 (Uyghur Geometry 7) is a fragment
(printed pp. 39–141 only); both works are held complete in
their Russian editions. Band E file 304 (Smirnov Geometry 8,
Kazakh) is cut at printed p. 143 of 160; the work is held
complete in Russian, Uzbek and Uyghur. The two Soltan
Grade 9 works in Band F (W4 algebra, W7 geometry) each lack
only the final colophon page in the held scans. The
Band C Grade 5 Atamura scans open on the title page with no
cover or flyleaf, so the primes-under-1,000 flyleaf table
that §2.2 tells pupils to use is not held in those copies.
The NIS *Mathematics* Grade 8 Part 1 is held in Kazakh only
(twice — the set's one true duplicate pair, files
1077 = 1078); its Russian edition is not held.

**Record corrections found by the covers (library-side
fixes owed):**

*Band A (Grades 1–2):*
1. Files 983/984 (Ospanov Grade 2 Parts 1–2), recorded as
   Russian, are the **Kazakh** editions (ISBNs
   978-601-10-0052-9 / 978-601-10-0051-2) — the "held
   twice" pair is a language pair, not re-scans.
2. Files 1204/1205 (Akpaeva Grade 1 Parts 1–2), recorded
   as Russian, are the **Uyghur** editions (print run
   1,850).
3. Files 12/13/625/626: the record's titles call them
   textbooks; their covers prove they are the Zhakupova
   Grade 1 **workbooks** №1–№4 (the record's kind field
   was right, its titles wrong).
4. Files 3/4/623/624 (Zhakupova Grade 1) are the
   **large-print special edition for visually impaired
   pupils** (adapted from the NIS 2016 textbook, Almaty
   2018) — the record does not say so. The set is held
   split by language: student books in Kazakh only, their
   workbooks in Russian only.

*Band B (Grades 3–4):*
5. Files 1233–1236, recorded as Russian, are **Kazakh**
   (2023 reprints of Grade 3 Parts 1–4); files 1237–1240,
   recorded as Russian, are **Uyghur** (2018); files
   1257–1260, recorded as Kazakh, are **Uyghur** (2019,
   Grade 4).
6. Grade 4 exists in **two editions** — 2019 and a 2020
   revised edition (reprinted 2023) — which the record
   does not distinguish.
7. The Grade 4 teacher books (files 719/720) print **no
   ISBN** anywhere in the book.

*Band C (Grades 5–6):*
8. Files 1392/1352, recorded as Kazakh, are the **Uyghur**
   editions of Mektep Grade 5; files 1375/1445 are the
   **Uyghur** and files 1450/1380 the **Uzbek** editions
   of Mektep Grade 6 — the record calls all of them
   Kazakh.
9. File 1558, recorded as a student's book under author
   "Абылкасымова Т.", is the Kazakh edition of the
   electronic **teacher** supplement to Mektep Grade 6;
   the author is А. Е. Әбілқасымова (Russian imprints:
   Абылкасымова) — one person and one series under two
   language spellings of the surname, settled by the
   identical colophon full name (see Band C's opening
   section).

*Band D (Grade 7):*
10. Files 1053–1058, recorded as a Russian "Algebra" by
    "Zabara", are the NIS integrated **Mathematics**
    course for NIS schools — five authors, Parts 1–2, in
    Kazakh, Russian **and English** (Astana, 2018).
11. File 1449, recorded as "Algebra and beginnings of
    analysis", is plain **Algebra 7** — the Uzbek edition
    of the 2017 Mektep textbook. No analysis content.
12. Files 1639/1640: the record's author "Kucher T.P."
    (alone) is wrong — 1639 is by Kucher, Zhumagulova and
    Korchevsky; 1640 is by Zhumagulova and Zhumalieva,
    and Kucher is not an author of it.
13. Files 1578/1579: the record's single authors are
    wrong — both are multi-author Astana-Kitap English
    books (Yelemessov et al., Algebra Grade 7, 2022;
    Turapbekov et al., Geometry Grade 7, 2022).

*Band E (Grade 8):*
14. Files 1077–1080, recorded as "Algebra" by
    Ryskeldieva, are the NIS combined **Mathematics**
    Grade 8 (algebra + geometry); Ryskeldieva is fourth
    of four–five authors.
15. Files 1383/1397 and 1357/1421, each recorded as two
    copies of one book, are **Uyghur/Uzbek edition pairs**
    (different translators, different ISBNs), not
    duplicates.
16. File 1584, recorded under "Yelemessov A.", is led by
    **Turapbekov A.** (six authors in all).

*Band F (Grades 9–10):*
17. The record leaves the language blank for most Grade 9
    files: covers prove files 385–391 are the **Kazakh**
    editions and files 433–439 their **Russian**
    counterparts.
18. File 573: the record does not state the track — it is
    the **humanities-track** Geometry 10 (see the gap
    statement above).
19. File 737: recorded as a Grade 10 textbook — it is a
    **worked-solutions manual** for a textbook not in
    hand, with its ISBN field printed blank (see above).
20. File 1586 is a distinct, later work — Yelemessov et
    al., *Algebra Grade 9*, in **English** (Astana-kitap,
    2023) — not an edition of any other Grade 9 algebra
    book in the set.

## Kazakhstan MATHS — Band A: Grades 1–2 (44 files, 4,880 PDF pages)

First read of the maths phase. Books are in Kazakh (KZ), Russian (RU) and — unexpectedly — Uyghur (UY). All summaries below are in English, written from the books themselves; only very short fragments are quoted. Printed page numbers are given; where the PDF page differs it is noted.

### How this band was read (method note)

- Every file's cover, title page and imprint page were inspected (text layer first; rendered pages where the PDF is image-only).
- Books with a usable text layer were read whole through that layer (Akpaeva G1 clean copies; Zhakupova G1 workbooks; Zhakupova G2 student's books and teacher's guides).
- Image-only books (Akpaeva G2 student's books and workbooks, Ospanov G2, Zhakupova G1 student's books, and the poor-OCR Akpaeva G1 copies) were read from their printed contents pages in full plus rendered sample spreads of every section (section openers, new-method lessons, self-check spreads). Their topic order below follows the printed contents exactly.
- Re-scan clusters were settled by comparing imprints (methodist/editor, date signed to print, print run, printer), contents and interior pages — not filenames.

### Clustering rule applied

(a) Same edition + language + part in several files = ONE work (printings/re-scans listed under it).
(b) Kazakh/Russian (and Uyghur) editions of the same series + part = one work filed once, all language editions listed; contents spot-checked across languages.
(c) Different years/editions = distinct works.
(d) Workbooks and teacher's guides = separate entries, each with a companion verdict.

## GRADE 1

### W1 — Akpaeva et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Part 1 (Quarters I–II, Lessons 1–64)

- **As printed:** Ақпаева Асель Бакировна, Лебедева Лариса Анатольевна, Мынжасарова Маржангул Жангазиновна, Лихобабенко Татьяна Викторовна. *Математика. Учебник для учащихся 1 класса общеобразовательной школы. Часть 1.* Almaty: ТОО «АЛМАТЫКІТАП БАСПАСЫ», 2021 (cover year; recommended by the Ministry of Education and Science of RK). Signed to print 28.06.2021 (clean copies); the RU clean copy is run 100,000, printed by PROMAT, Istanbul, while the KZ clean copy is an additional run of 91,000, printed by İmak Ofset, Istanbul.
- **ISBNs as printed:** RU edition — set 978-601-01-4425-5, Part 1: 978-601-01-4422-4. KZ edition — set 978-601-01-4565-8, Part 1: 978-601-01-4569-6. (UY edition ISBN not legible in the held scan; imprint is the standard bilingual block.)
- **Files:** 1189 (KZ, 140 PDF pp, poor OCR layer), 926 (KZ, 140 pp, clean text), 1191 (RU, 140 pp, poor OCR), 915 (RU, 140 pp, clean text), 1204 (UY, 140 pp, poor OCR). Printed page = PDF page in the clean copies.
- **Structure:** 132 numbered lessons across Parts 1–2, grouped by quarter with a lexical theme per quarter (Q1 "All about me. My school"; Q2 "My family and friends. The world around us"). Every lesson is a 2-page spread with a fixed rubric set: new material / pair work / group work / independent work / "You are a researcher" / "Maths in life", plus a "Remember!" (Запомни!) box stating the rule.

**Teaching order and content (printed pp.):**
- *Counting and comparison first, numerals gradually.* L1 Counting objects (p.6); L2 Comparing groups of objects — equal/more/less, signs `=`, `<`, `>` (p.8). Then one number at a time: number and digit 1 (p.10); 2 with composition of 2 (p.12); 3 with composition of 3 (p.14); **addition and subtraction are introduced at pp.16–18, before the numbers are finished** — from pictures of joining/separating sets, with the component names taught immediately (слагаемое/слагаемое/значение суммы; уменьшаемое/вычитаемое/значение разности, p.17). Digits continue: 4 & composition (p.20), 5 & composition (p.22), counting to 5 (p.24), **0 — including adding/subtracting with zero** (p.26), 6 (p.28) + composition (p.30), 7 (p.32) + composition (p.34), 8 (p.36) + composition (p.38), 9 (p.40) + composition (p.42), 10 (p.44) + composition of 10 (p.46), addition/subtraction within 10 (pp.48–50). "Composition of a number" (состав числа) — all part-pairs of each number — is the core memorisation device throughout.
- *Geometry and quantities interleaved (pp.52–68):* figures, point, lines (p.52); ray and segment (p.54); properties of objects and figures (p.56); longer/shorter, wider/narrower (p.58); measuring length, centimetre (p.60); decimetre (p.62); heavier/lighter, kilogram (p.64); litre (p.66); measuring quantities (p.68).
- *Q2 — systematic addition/subtraction within 10 (pp.72–106):* commutative property of addition (p.72); adding/subtracting the number 2 (p.74), 3 (p.76), 2 and 3 (p.78), 4 (p.80), 5 (p.82), review (p.84), 6 (p.86), 7 (p.88), 8 and 9 (p.90); subtracting from 10 (p.92); fluency lessons (pp.94–106).
- *Tens (pp.96–100, inside the same run):* counting in tens — a ten as a larger counting unit, names 10–100 with tens/ones table (p.96); adding and subtracting whole tens (pp.98–100).
- *Patterns and halves (pp.108–116):* sequences of numbers and figures (p.108); **half of the even numbers 2, 4, 6, 8, 10**, where even/odd are also named (p.110); puzzles and rebuses (pp.112–116).
- *Time and consolidation (pp.118–132):* measuring geometric figures (p.118); time, the hour — clock face, hour hand (p.120); measuring and weighing (p.122); quantities review (p.124); three consolidation lessons and a two-lesson generalisation (pp.126–132). Glossary at p.134 (printed): defines e.g. decimetre, kilogram, litre, ray, segment, sequence, difference.

**Methods/algorithms:** counting on from pictures and fingers; composition-of-number tables as the addition/subtraction engine (no counting-on algorithm is formalised); comparison by pairing groups; measurement with a ruler in cm/dm; telling time to the hour only.
**Worked examples / problem types:** picture-based join/separate stories read off as `2+1=3` / `3–1=2`; component-name drills; missing-number cards; ordering numbers; "researcher" pattern tasks. No formal word-problem schema yet (that arrives in Part 2).
**Honest negatives:** no multiplication or division; no numbers above 100 and no two-digit numbers except whole tens; no written (column) algorithms; no perimeter/area; no money; no equations (only missing-number boxes in expressions); fractions appear only as "half" of small even numbers.
**Defects:** files 1189/1191/1204 have a rotated, largely unusable OCR layer (watermark + imprint only) — content verified against the clean twins 926/915 and covers. All five files end with imprint + publisher's page, i.e. complete.

### W2 — Akpaeva et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Part 2 (Quarters III–IV, Lessons 65–132)

- **As printed:** same authors/publisher, 2021; signed to print 01.07.2021 — RU clean copy run 100,000, PROMAT; KZ clean copy run 200,000, İmak Ofset. ISBN RU — Part 2: 978-601-01-4428-6 (set 978-601-01-4425-5); KZ — Part 2: 978-601-01-4567-2 (set 978-601-01-4565-8). Files: 1190 (KZ), 927 (KZ), 1192 (RU), 916 (RU), 1205 (UY); 152 PDF pp each; printed p. = PDF p.

**Teaching order and content:**
- *Q3 "Journey. Traditions and folklore" — word problems become the centre (pp.6–40):* addition and subtraction as mutually inverse operations (p.6); **the word problem (задача) is defined at p.10** — condition, question, solution, answer, taught with a worked picture example and a schematic record; a long run of problem-solving lessons by type (pp.12–40) including **inverse problems** (обратные задачи, pp.16, 28) and composing problems.
- *Time and calendar (pp.42–48):* seasons, months, days of the week (p.42); hour and minute (p.44); measuring quantities; quantities review (pp.46–48).
- *First algebra (pp.50–76):* numerical and **letter expressions** (pp.50–52); equalities and inequalities (p.54); comparing numerical expressions (p.56); equalities with an unknown (p.58); **the equation (уравнение)** — defined as an equality with an unknown, unknown denoted by a letter, root of the equation, solving by selection and by the addition/subtraction relationship, with mandatory checking (pp.60–64); mixed lessons (pp.66–76) closing with "What do you know and can do?" / "What have you learned?" checks.
- *Q4 "Food and drink. A healthy mind in a healthy body" — numbers 11–20 (pp.82–98):* numbers 11–20 over five lessons; composition of 11–20; addition and subtraction within 20 (including across the ten, pp.88–94); counting in groups of 2 to 20 (p.96); comparing numbers to 20 (p.98).
- *Money (pp.100–110):* coin denominations (тенге coins) (p.100); denomination and price (p.102); buying and change (p.104); more/less expensive (p.106); exchanging coins and paying (pp.108–110).
- *Data, sets, logic, geometry (pp.112–144):* problem lessons (p.112); tables and pictograms (pp.114–116); diagrams (p.118); problems (pp.120–122); **sets (множества)** — set, element, empty set, equal/unequal sets (p.124); constructing segments (pp.126–128); logic problems (p.130); problem lessons (pp.132–134); geometric figures (pp.136–140); final checks (pp.142–144). Glossary p.146 adds: statement (true/false), two-digit number, money, diagram, text problem, value of an expression, set, coin, inequality.

**Methods:** word problems are modelled with a short record (brief notation) and a scheme; equations solved by trial and by inverse-operation reasoning, always with a check; money work is practical (making amounts, change from coins).
**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division; no addition/subtraction above 20 except whole tens (Part 1); no column written methods; no perimeter; time only to hour/minute recognition, no elapsed-time calculation; no fractions beyond Part 1's "half".
**Defects:** same OCR situation as W1 (1190/1192/1205 poor layers; 927/916 clean). Complete with imprints.

### W3 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Part 1 (NIS textbook, large-print special edition)

- **As printed:** Г.Ш. Жакупова, Н.В. Орехова, Н.В. Лебедева, С.И. Уакбаева, А.О. Мадхалыкова, Н.З. Иманбаева. *Математика. 1-сынып. Оқулық* — textbook for Grade 1 of special general schools (classes) for children with impaired vision (visually impaired). Commissioned by the Ministry of Education and Science of RK; © АОО «Назарбаев Интеллектуальные школы» ДББУ, 2016; this large-print adaptation printed by ЖШС «ӘБЕО орталығы», Almaty (Baizakov St. 273B), signed to print 11.06.2018, format 60×90. **ISBN 978-601-249-313-9 (set); 978-601-249-312-2 (Part 1).** Language: Kazakh. File: student_3, 132 PDF pp.
- **Record correction:** the set-table title does not mention that this is the special (large-print, visually-impaired) edition; the cover/title page proves it. The title-verso bibliographic record says "158 б." (158 pp.) but the held PDF has 132 pages and ends with the imprint — flagged as a discrepancy (either the record counts a different issue or the PDF lacks pages; the contents in the PDF run to the section end, p.128 topic + imprint).

**Teaching order (printed contents, pp.):** Section 1 *Numbers and digits*: counting objects (p.6); ordinal counting (p.8); comparing numbers (p.14); the number line (сандық сәуле, p.18); more/less/equal (p.22); then number-and-digit lessons one by one — 1 (p.28), 2 (p.32), 3 (p.38), 4 (p.42), 5 (p.46), 6 (p.50), 7 (p.56), 8 (p.62), 9 (p.66), 0 (p.70), the number 10 (p.74); how 10 can be obtained (p.78); counting forwards and backwards (p.82); adding and subtracting one-digit numbers (p.86); "how many more? how many fewer?" (p.90). Section 2 *Geometric figures*: how objects differ (1) (p.94), (2) (p.97); point, line, ray (p.100); segment, broken line (p.104); solid (spatial) figures (p.108); flat figures (p.110); models of flat figures (1) (p.112), (2) (p.116). Section 3 (begun) *Quantities and measures*: comparing objects by length (p.120); how do we measure length? (p.124); comparing objects by mass (p.128).
**Lesson format:** each unit opens with a "You will learn…" (Сен … үйренесің) goal box and key words (Тірек сөздер); big-print, picture-led tasks; worked problem models use bar schemas (сызба).
**Honest negatives (Pt1):** no addition/subtraction tables yet, no numbers above 10, no money, no time, no equations; addition/subtraction appear only as one-digit operations at the section end.
**Companion:** Workbook №1 (W7) matches this part's opening (worksheets 1–62 run from counting to mass comparison).

### W4 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Part 2

- **As printed:** same team/edition as W3; ISBN 978-601-249-313-9 (set); 978-601-249-314-6 (Part 2). KZ. File: student_4, 134 PDF pp.
**Teaching order (contents):** Section 3 (continued) *Quantities and measures*: how do we measure mass? (p.6); comparing objects by volume (p.10); how do we measure volume? (p.14); quantities and their properties (p.18); mathematical roundabout/games (p.22). Section 4 *Adding and subtracting numbers to 20*: building the addition table (p.26); the addition table (p.29); the addition table as a square (p.32); the relationship between addition and subtraction (p.36); computing with the addition table (p.39) — the sampled spread (printed pp.30–31) shows the table built column by column and a "postman" game finding the "addresses" (row/column) of sums in the table; the commutative property of addition (p.43); **two-digit numbers** (p.46); comparing two-digit numbers (p.50); counting in tens (p.54); adding and subtracting round numbers (p.57); adding/subtracting with the number line (p.62); adding numbers within 20 (p.67); subtracting within 20 (p.70); the numerical expression (p.74). Section 5 *Numbers and patterns*: counting by twos (p.78); counting by adding/subtracting twos (p.81); half of a number (p.85); patterns (1) (p.88), (2) (p.92); puzzle tasks (p.95); puzzles and rebuses (p.99). Section 6 *Measuring quantities*: measuring length (p.102); measuring the sides of a rectangle (p.105); measuring the sides of a square (p.108); quantities: mass (p.112); quantities: volume (p.116); measures around us (p.119); winter mathematical games (p.124). Glossary p.128.
**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division; no money or time yet (they are in Pt3–4); no equations yet; numbers capped at 20 plus round tens.
**Companion:** Workbook №2 (W8) continues the worksheet numbering and matches the back half of this part and beyond (mass/volume sheets 63–66 open it — see W8 note on alignment).

### W5 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Part 3

- **As printed:** same team/edition; ISBN set 978-601-249-313-9; the Pt3 bibliographic record seen for the workbook line gives 978-601-249-315-3 (Part 3, student's book record on the title verso of file 623). KZ. File: student_623, 118 PDF pp.
**Teaching order (contents):** Section 1 *Operations on numbers*: comparing one- and two-digit numbers to 20 (opening lesson, p.5); expressions in two operations (p.9); order of operations in expressions with brackets (p.13); inverse operations (p.16). Section 2 *Quantities. Telling the time*: ordering events by stage (p.20); quantities (p.20); telling/gauging time (p.20); the minute (p.28); the hour (p.32); determining the time (p.36); days of the week (p.40); the month (p.44); length, mass, volume review (p.47). Section 3 *Equality and inequality. The equation*: equality and inequality (p.50); true and untrue equalities (p.54); an equality with an unknown (p.58); parts and the whole (p.62); the letter expression (p.66); the value of the letter in a letter expression (p.68); comparing numerical expressions (p.71); comparing letter expressions (p.74); **the equation** (p.78); solving an equation with an unknown addend (p.81); with an unknown subtrahend (pp.84, 87); with an unknown minuend (pp.90, 93); solving equations (p.96); **what is a problem?** (p.99); the graphical model of a problem (p.102); problems on addition (p.105); problems on subtraction (p.108); the inverse problem (p.111); composing and solving inverse problems (p.114).
**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division; no money yet; arithmetic stays within 20 (plus tens work from Pt2); no perimeter.
**Companion:** Workbook №3 (W9) restarts worksheet numbering at 1 with "Comparing numbers to 20" — it matches this part's opening.

- **As printed:** same team/edition; ISBN set 978-601-249-313-9; 978-601-249-316-0 (Part 4). KZ. File: student_624, 122 PDF pp.
**Teaching order (contents):** Section 3 (continued): problems on increasing/decreasing a number by several units (p.5); problems on difference comparison (p.8); solving various problems (p.11); "Mathematical Nauryz" (p.14). (p.32); trade/shopping problems (p.35); expensive–cheap (p.39); sets and elements of logic (p.42). (p.62); comparing sets (p.65); equal sets (p.65); set, the empty set (p.69); making combinations from objects (p.73); building combinations (p.76); length — measure and compare (p.79); mass — measure and compare (p.82); volume — measure and compare (p.85). Section 6 *Position and direction of objects*: straight line, segment, broken line (p.91); measuring segments, drawing figures (p.94); drawing figures on dotted paper (p.97); a model of figures on dotted paper (p.100); comparing figures (p.103); position of figures (p.106); position of objects (p.109); mathematical marathon (p.111). ### W7 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Workbook (Рабочая тетрадь) №1 — RU

- **As printed:** *Математика: Рабочая тетрадь №1* for Grade 1 of special schools (classes) for visually impaired pupils, in 4 parts; authors as W3 (with А.О. Мадхалыкова); prepared with «Мектеп»; signed to print 11.06.2018 (imprint). **ISBN 978-601-249-322-1.** RU. File: workbook_12, 82 PDF pp.
- **Record correction:** the set-table title calls this file a "учебник" (textbook); the cover and imprint prove it is the workbook. The record's kind field (workbook) was right; its title was wrong. Same correction applies to W8–W10.
- **Content:** worksheets ("Рабочий лист") 1–62, each a 1–2 page spread of write-in tasks: counting objects and matching to numeral cards (sheet 1), ordinal counting with colouring instructions (sheet 3), and so on through the Pt1 student's book topics (numbers/digits, comparison, geometry, length/mass).
- **Companion verdict:** companion to the Zhakupova G1 student's book — but note the language split in the held set: student's books are held in Kazakh, workbooks in Russian. Worksheet topics track the student's book sections in order; the workbook is a practice/write-in companion, not a substitute textbook (tasks presuppose the lesson).

### W9 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Workbook №3 — RU

- **Companion verdict:** companion to student's Part 3 (and the problem strand that continues into Part 4).

### W10 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 1, Workbook №4 — RU

- **As printed:** **ISBN 978-601-249-325-2.** File: workbook_626, 94 PDF pp. Worksheets 66–136 (continues №3): opens with inverse problems (sheet 66) and difference-comparison problems (sheet 67), matching student's Part 4 problem strand; includes money, sets/logic and position/direction practice; ends with pattern/colouring review spreads.

## GRADE 2

### W11 — Akpaeva et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 1 (Almatykitap, 2018)

- **As printed:** Ақпаева А.Б., Лебедева Л.А., Мынжасарова М.Ж. (three authors — Likhobabenko is not on the Grade 2 set). *Математика. Жалпы білім беретін мектептің 2-сынып оқушыларына арналған оқулық. 1-бөлім.* Алматыкітап баспасы, 2018 (title page); methodist Ф.С. Лекерова; KZ imprint: signed to print 18.06.2018, additional run 15,000, printed by Imak Ofset, Istanbul; the book carries a "+CD" mark. Files: student_33 (KZ, 73 PDF pp), student_43 (RU, 73 PDF pp). Image-only scans. Printed page ≈ PDF page − 1 in the KZ copy (contents on PDF pp.70–71).
- **Lesson format:** thematic units ("My family and friends" etc.); each part ends with a "Өзіңді тексер!" (Check yourself!) spread organised by Bloom levels — Білу (know), Түсіну (understand), Қолдану (apply), Талдау (analyse), Жинақтау (synthesise), Бағалау (evaluate) — a format used in all four parts.

**Teaching order (printed contents, KZ Part 1):**
- *Section 1A — Two-digit numbers (pp.6–14):* review of 11–20 and full tens; the number 100 (p.6); two-digit numbers (p.8); place-value composition of two-digit numbers (p.10); comparing two-digit numbers by place (p.12); self-check (p.14).
- *Section 1B — Operations with numbers. Problems (pp.16–38):* adding across the ten (p.16); subtracting across the ten (p.18); then the composition of each number in turn — 11 (p.20), consolidation (p.22), 12 (p.24), consolidation (p.26), 13 (p.28), 14 (p.30), 15 and 16 (p.32), 17 and 18 (p.34), the addition/subtraction table for the composition of 20 (p.36), consolidation (p.38). The across-ten method is the standard "complete the ten first" decomposition, driven by memorised compositions.
- *Section 1C — Quantities and their units (pp.60–68 in the contents' second page, after the family-theme problem lessons pp.42–56 on oral addition/subtraction within 100 in two-operation problems):* measuring instruments (p.60); the metre (p.62); the centner (p.64); performing operations on quantities (p.66) — the self-check spread (printed pp.68–69) sets e.g. adding dm and cm, m and cm, kg differences and a word problem about 65 kg of carrots (a storehouse held 1 centner of carrots, 65 kg were used); self-check (p.68).

**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division; no written column algorithm yet (that is Part 2); no hundreds beyond the number 100 itself; no time, money or sets (Parts 2–3); no perimeter.
**Defects:** both files are image-only (watermark-only text layer); covers/contents/imprints rendered and read; interior sampled at section openers and self-check spreads.

### W12 — Akpaeva et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 2

- **As printed:** same 2018 edition; files student_646 (KZ, 73 pp), student_649 (RU, 73 pp, cover verified "Часть 2").
**Teaching order (contents):** Theme "My school". *Section 2A — Adding and subtracting two-digit numbers. Hundreds. Problems (pp.6–34):* Roman numerals and hundreds (p.6); counting to 100 (p.8); the hundred as a new counting unit (p.10); adding/subtracting hundreds (p.12); adding/subtracting quantities (p.14); solving problems in convenient ways (p.16); **the written (column) addition/subtraction algorithm for two-digit numbers** (p.18), introduced on the cases 32+45, 77−32, with further case lessons — 46+14, 60−14, 82+18, 100−82 (p.20); addition/subtraction (p.22); problems (p.24); the algorithm on the cases 15+28, 43−15 (p.26); problems (p.28); consolidation (p.30); problems (p.32); self-check (p.34). *Section 2B — Quantities (pp.38–48):* the day (тәулік) (p.38); time and the day (p.40); quantities (pp.42–44); time and its units (p.46); self-check (p.48). *Section 2C — The set and denoting its elements. The signs ∈ and ∉ (pp.50–68):* the signs ∈ and ∉ (p.50); uniting sets and splitting into parts (p.52); intersection of sets (p.54); true and false statements (pp.56–58); making combinations "three at a time" (p.60); collecting data (p.62); consolidation (p.64); tables and diagrams (p.66); self-check (p.68). The Part 2 self-check (printed p.69) includes checking a bar diagram built from a table and set-membership tasks with numbers to 600.
**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division yet; Roman numerals only as a notation topic (used on clock faces); no perimeter; no fractions.

### W13 — Akpaeva et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 3

- **As printed:** same 2018 edition; KZ imprint of this part signed 18.06.2019 (additional run 16,000) in the held copy — a later printing, same edition. Files: student_647 (KZ, 89 PDF pp), student_650 (RU, 89 pp).
**Teaching order (contents):** Theme "A sound mind in a sound body". *Section 3A — Geometric figures and their arrangement (pp.6–16):* types of angles; polygons (p.6); classifying polygons; rectangle and square (p.8); building figures (pp.10–12); sequences of geometric figures (p.14); self-check (p.16). *Section 3B — Multiplication and division. Problems (pp.18–42):* counting in equal groups; dividing into equal groups (p.18); **multiplication** (p.20) — introduced (sampled spread, printed pp.20–21) as repeated addition over picture arrays, with the terms multiplier/multiplicand/product (көбейткіш, көбейткіш, көбейтіндінің мәні) and the × sign (also printed "x"); division (p.22); the commutative property of multiplication (p.24); the relationship between the components of multiplication and division (p.26); multiplication and division tables for 2 (pp.28–30), 3 (pp.32–34), 4 (p.36), 5 (p.40); building and consolidating the tables for 2–5 (p.42). Theme "Customs and folklore": tenge, coins and paper money (p.46); price and cost (p.48); sets and their elements (p.50); self-check (p.52). *Section 3C — Numerical and letter expressions. Equality and inequality (pp.54–84):* equality and inequality; numerical and letter expressions (p.54); operations with the number 1 (p.56); consolidation (p.58); equations on multiplication and division (p.60), solving them (p.62); solving complex (two-step) equations (p.64); problems on increasing/decreasing a number several times (pp.66–68) and problem lessons (pp.70–74); problems in direct and indirect form (p.76); problem lessons (pp.78–80); consolidation (p.82); self-check (p.84). The self-check spread (printed pp.84–85) sets bracket equations such as y + (12 − 9) = 58 (form paraphrased), inverse-problem composition and two-step problems.
**Honest negatives:** multiplication/division tables only up to 5; no division with remainder; no area or perimeter yet (Part 4); no fractions; no three-digit written algorithms.

### W14 — Akpaeva et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 4

- **As printed:** same 2018 edition; files student_648 (KZ, 73 pp), student_651 (RU, 73 pp).
**Teaching order (contents):** Theme "The environment around us". *Section 4A — Efficient ways of computing (pp.6–20):* efficient computation (p.6); order of operations in expressions (p.8); consolidation lessons (pp.10–18); self-check (p.20). Theme "Journey". *Section 4B — Ways of solving problems (pp.24–36):* solving problems (p.24); compound (two-step) problems (pp.26–32); consolidation (p.34); self-check (p.36). *Section 4C — Geometric figures. Perimeter. Area (pp.38–68):* **perimeter of the triangle, rectangle and square** (p.38); building figures (pp.40–44); direction of movement (p.46); position of objects (pp.48–50); geometry-content problems (pp.52–54); logic problems (pp.56–58); consolidation (pp.60–62); self-check (p.64); "What do I know?" (p.66); a game for memorising the multiplication table (p.68).
**Honest negatives:** although the Section 4C heading includes the word "Аудан" (area), **no area-computation lesson appears in the printed contents** — the geometry lessons are perimeter, construction, direction and position only. No fractions; no division with remainder; tables stop at 5.

### W15–W18 — Akpaeva et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Workbooks №1–№4

- **As printed:** Ақпаева Ә.Б., Лебедева Л.А., Мынжасарова М.Ж. *Математика. №1–№4 жұмыс дәптері / Рабочая тетрадь №1–№4* — workbooks for the Grade 2 textbook, Алматыкітап, 2017 (bibliographic records on the symbols pages: №1 — 56 printed pp., ISBN 978-601-01-3103-3 (KZ №1); №3 — 92 printed pp., and the held KZ №3 prints the same ISBN 978-601-01-3103-3 on its symbols page as №1, №2 and №4 — the one number is repeated in every part, as printed). Held PDFs run ~4 pages longer than the printed books (scan wrappers/covers). Files: №1 — workbook_835 (KZ, 60 PDF pp) + workbook_839 (RU, 60 pp); №2 — 836 (KZ, 80) + 840 (RU, 80); №3 — 837 (KZ, 96) + 841 (RU, 96); №4 — 838 (KZ, 68) + 842 (RU, 68). Covers verified: KZ covers read "№… жұмыс дәптері", RU "Рабочая тетрадь №…".
- **Content and companion verdict:** each workbook is lesson-keyed to the student's book of the same number (workbook lessons carry the year's continuous lesson numbers: Workbook №1 opens with "1-сабақ" on numbers 0–10, 11–20, full tens and 100, with a maths-dictation (математикалық диктант) list and a printed 10-point grading scale; Workbook №3's sampled lessons include №65 "Types of angles. Polygons", matching student's Part 3). Task types: write-in computation (including column form), completing tables/schemas, drawing/building figures, self-assessment scales. **Verdict: true companions to W11–W14 respectively, in both languages; they practise the same lessons in the same order and add no new topics.** The 2017 workbook imprint year vs the 2018 student's book is as printed (the workbook set was issued for the same renewed programme).
- **Defects:** all eight files are image-only scans; contents verified from covers, symbols/ISBN pages and sampled lesson pages.

### W19 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 1 (NIS, 2022) — RU

- **As printed:** Г.Ш. Жакупова, Н.В. Лебедева, Н.В. Орехова, Е.В. Ушакова. *Математика. Учебник для 2-го класса общеобразовательной школы: в 4 ч. Часть 1.* Нур-Султан: АОО «Назарбаев Интеллектуальные школы», 2022 — 112 pp. **ISBN 978-601-348-354-2 (Part 1); 978-601-348-361-0 (set).** File: student_1024, 112 PDF pp, full text layer. (The CIP block as printed shows both "ББК 22.1я71" and, lower, "ББК 51я71" — a printed inconsistency in the book itself.)
- **Format:** quarter = part. Every lesson has "You will learn…" goals, key words, and rubriced tasks: Реши (practise), Попробуй (apply), Подумай (think/logic), Запомни! (remember), Знаешь ли ты? (extra). QR codes link to an online trainer at section ends. Mascots (Линейка, Циркуль, Ластик — Ruler, Compass, Eraser) carry the narrative.

**Teaching order:** *Q1, Section 1 — Two-digit numbers:* reading/writing two-digit numbers on the number line (Lesson 1); place composition — 38 = 3 tens + 8 units = 30 + 8 (L2); graphic (tens/units block) models (L3); comparing two-digit numbers, tens first then units (L4); counting patterns within 100. *Section 2 — Operations with numbers. Problems:* adding one-digit numbers across the ten; commutative and associative properties of addition; expressions with and without brackets; modelling two-step problems with schemes and brief records; compound (составные) problems. Methods shown in the sampled lessons: adding by parts via completing the ten (the book's worked example is 9 + 6 = (9 + 1) + 5, printed p.24), graphic models, and equations from the very first lessons (x + 12 = 18 type) as practice items. *Section 3 — Quantities and their units:* reading scales of measuring instruments; metre and centner; comparing quantity values and adding/subtracting them; converting length and mass units.
**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division in Part 1; no hundreds beyond counting; no written column algorithm as a named method (that is Part 2); no money/time/sets yet.
**Companion:** Teacher's Guide Part 1 (W25) covers this part and Part 2.

### W20 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 2 — RU

- **As printed:** same series, 2022 — 112 pp. **ISBN 978-601-348-355-9 (Part 2).** File: student_1025.
**Teaching order:** *Q2, Section 1 — Addition and subtraction of two-digit numbers. Hundreds:* addition of the type 45 + 9 and subtraction 45 − 9, taught by the **by-parts method** and with graphic models (sampled: 35 − 8 = 35 − 5 − 3); consolidation across the ten; **the hundred — counting in hundreds to 1000**; adding/subtracting hundreds; number patterns to 100 and in hundreds to 1000; column (столбиком) addition/subtraction is named in the section goals; properties of addition and rational methods; problem lessons. *Section 2 — Quantities:* **Roman numerals 1–12** (including on clock faces); time to hours and minutes; the day (сутки); metre, centner; converting units; adding/subtracting quantity values. *Section 3 — Denoting a set and its elements. The signs ∈ and ∉:* composing number sets by a feature; set/element notation and diagrams; union and intersection of two sets; true and false statements; combining objects "three at a time".
**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division yet; no three-digit numbers except round hundreds; no perimeter.

### W21 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 3 — RU

- **As printed:** same series, 2022 — 128 pp. **ISBN 978-601-348-356-6 (Part 3).** File: student_1026.
**Teaching order:** *Q3, Section 1 — Geometric figures and their relative position:* the angle — vertex, sides; right/acute/obtuse angles, building a right angle with a set square (sampled lessons 1–2); polygons — kinds, vertices, sides; building figures on dotted paper by instruction; sequences of geometric figures and finding errors in them. *Section 2 — Multiplication and division. Problems:* counting by 3, 4, 5 forwards/backwards to 50; multiplication as an operation (components named); division; multiplication tables for 2, 3, 4, 5 and using the table for division by 2–5; money problems with coins and banknotes (purchases); simple multiplication/division problems and their inverse problems. *Section 3 — Numerical and letter expressions. Equations. Problems:* equations on multiplication/division; complex equations with addition/subtraction; kinds of multiplication/division problems; schemes and brief records for compound problems; writing solutions by steps and as a single expression.
**Honest negatives:** tables only to 5; no perimeter yet (Part 4); no division with remainder; no fractions.

### W22 — Zhakupova et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 4 — RU

- **As printed:** same series, 2022 — 104 pp. **ISBN 978-601-348-359-7 (Part 4).** File: student_1027.
**Teaching order:** *Q4, Section 1 — Rational ways of computing:* properties of addition and multiplication (commutative/associative, stated with letter formulas a + b = b + a, (a + b) + c = a + (b + c), a·b = b·a) applied to convenient computation; **order of operations** — two lessons, (1) without brackets (× and ÷ before + and −, left to right) and (2) with brackets, each with fully worked numbered-step examples; comparing numerical and letter expressions. *Section 2 — Ways of solving problems:* problems on increasing/decreasing a number several times; **multiple (кратное) comparison** — "how many times more/fewer"; simple and compound multiplication/division problems. *Section 3 — Geometric figures. Perimeter:* **perimeter formulas for triangle, square and rectangle** and applying them; finding an unknown side from the perimeter; building flat figures on squared paper to a given perimeter; dividing figure models into parts and composing new figures; starting position and direction of turning a figure; logic problems; year review.
**Honest negatives (whole Zhakupova G2 set):** no area; no fractions; no division with remainder; no numbers beyond 1000 (hundreds only); no negative numbers; multiplication limited to the tables for 2–5 (with one-digit × one-digit facts used in expressions).

### W23 — Ospanov et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 1 (Atamura, 2022) — RU + KZ

- **As printed:** RU edition — Т.К. Оспанов, Ж.К. Астамбаева, Н.О. Мергенбаева, А.С. Козленко. *Математика. Для 2 кл. общеобразовательной шк. В 2 частях. Часть 1.* Алматы: Атамұра, 2022 — 144 pp. **ISBN 978-601-10-0043-7 (Part 1); 978-601-10-0030-7 (set).** KZ edition — Оспанов Т.Қ., Астамбаева Ж.Қ., Мергенбаева Н.О. (Kozlenko is not listed in the KZ edition as printed). *Математика. 1-бөлім.* Атамұра, 2022 — 144 pp. **ISBN 978-601-10-0052-9 (Part 1); 978-601-10-0032-1 (set).** Files: student_974 (RU, 144 PDF pp), student_983 (KZ, 144 pp) — **the record listed both as RU; the KZ file's title page proves it is the Kazakh edition (record correction).** Both files are image-only scans; read from full printed contents + sampled lesson spreads. Printed p. = PDF p.
- **Format:** 136 numbered lessons across the two parts (Lessons 1–64 in Part 1), each lesson 2 printed pages, with a fixed icon system (key words, lesson goal, new topic, review, entertaining problem, group/pair work, "Remember!", unknown-number box, homework). Fifteen numbered independent works (самостоятельные работы №1–8 in Part 1, №9–15 in Part 2) across the year act as tests. The sampled Lesson 1 spread (printed pp.4–5) shows the method: forming two-digit numbers by counting units and tens ("1 дес. + 1 ед. = 11"), the rule box "Read a two-digit number tens first, then units", and an explicit note that each digit's place is called its разряд (place), with digits 0–9 named "Arabic numerals".

**Teaching order (printed contents, Part 1):** *Two-digit numbers (Lessons 1–6):* forming two-digit numbers, counting in tens (L1, p.4); writing/reading (L2); place-value components (разрядные слагаемые, L3); graphic models (L4); comparing (L5); independent work №1 (L6). *Operations with numbers. Problems (L7–36):* expressions with and without brackets (L7); oral addition/subtraction within 100 without crossing the ten (L8–11); adding one-digit numbers across the ten (L12); subtracting across the ten (L13); the addition table across the ten (L14); mixed lessons (L15); oral ± within 100 with crossing (L16–18); methods of computing across the ten (L19); independent work №2 (L20); evaluating expressions by rational methods (L21–22); problems on finding the sum and addends (L23); on finding the remainder, minuend and subtrahend (L24); on increasing/decreasing by several units (L25); on difference comparison (L26); independent work №3 (L27). *Quantities (L28–36):* length and its units (L28); mass (L29); volume/capacity (L30); reading instrument scales (L31); adding/subtracting quantity values (L32); independent work №4 (L33); review lessons (L34–36). *Adding and subtracting two-digit numbers. Hundreds (L37–49):* **written** addition/subtraction of two-digit numbers without crossing (L37); **written** with crossing (L38); written subtraction with crossing (L39); the hundred — counting in hundreds, operations with numbers ending in zeros (L40); number sequences (L41); rational computation methods (L42); independent work №5 (L43); compound problems (L44–45); transforming simple problems into compound ones (L46); compound problems (L47); solving compound problems by different methods (L48); independent work №6 (L49). *Quantities again (L50–55):* Roman numerals (L50); time and its units (L51); telling time by the clock face (L52); converting and comparing quantity units (L53); adding/subtracting quantity values (L54); independent work №7 (L55). *Denoting a set and its elements. The signs ∈ and ∉ (L56–64):* composing and classifying sets (L56); composing/classifying number sets (L57); union and intersection of two sets (L58–59); true and false statements (L60); combinations of objects "three at a time" (L61); independent work №8 (L62); review/generalisation (L63–64). Reference material p.129; glossary p.135.

**Honest negatives:** no multiplication/division in Part 1; no perimeter; Roman numerals limited to clock-style use; no fractions.

### W24 — Ospanov et al., Matematika, Grade 2, Part 2 — RU + KZ

- **As printed:** RU — *Часть 2*, Атамұра, 2022, 144 pp. (part ISBN 978-601-10-0042-0; set 978-601-10-0030-7). KZ — *2-бөлім*, **ISBN 978-601-10-0051-2 (Part 2); set 978-601-10-0032-1.** Files: student_975 (RU), student_984 (KZ; record said RU — correction as in W23).
**Teaching order (contents, Lessons 65–136):** *Geometric figures and their relative position (L65–70):* kinds of angles and polygons (L65); classifying geometric figures (L66); classifying polygons (L67); constructing geometric figures (L68); sequences of geometric figures and numbers (L69); independent work №9 (L70). *Multiplication and division. Problems (L71–83):* uniting sets with equal numbers of elements — **multiplication** (L71, p.16); the components of multiplication (L72); dividing a set into equal parts — **division** (L73); components of division (L74); the mutual relationship between multiplication and division components (L75–76); multiplication and division as inverse operations (L77); the multiplication table for 2 (L78), 3 (L79), 4 (L80), 5 (L81); coins and paper money — operations with money (L82); independent work №10 (L83). Sampled division lesson (printed p.21): the sign ":" is introduced with its readings ("6 : 3 is read as '6 divided by 3'… 'in 6 there are 2 threes'"). *Numerical and letter expressions. Equations. Problems (L84–103):* order of operations in expressions (L84); numerical expressions (L85); letter expressions (L86); the properties of addition and multiplication (L87); properties of × and ÷ connected with 1 (L88); simple equations and inequalities (L89); equations of complex structure (L90); independent work №11 (L91); problem lessons (L92); problems on multiplying/dividing a number several times (L93); problems on finding an unknown multiplier/divisor etc. (L94–95); problems with an indirect question on ×/÷ (L96); problems on multiple comparison (L97); two-step problems (L98–101); independent work №12 (L102); review (L103). *Rational ways of computing (L104–110):* expressions with two or three operations (L105 — L104 is the review of lessons 103–104); comparing numerical expressions (L107); comparing letter expressions (L108); rational methods (L109); independent work №13 (L110). *Ways of solving problems (L111–118):* problems on increasing/decreasing a number several times and on multiple comparison (L111); problems with an indirect question on ×/÷ (L112); problem lessons (L113–117); independent work №14 (L118). *Geometric figures. Perimeter (L119–128):* denoting geometric figures with letters (L119); dividing models of flat figures into parts and assembling figures from parts (L120); measuring the sides of polygons (L121); **perimeter formulas for the rectangle, square and triangle** (L122) — the sampled lesson (printed p.117) works P = a + b + c for a triangle and applies formulas for rectangle/square perimeters, including word problems; dependence between quantities (L123); problems on finding a side and the perimeter of a rectangle/square (L124); constructing figures to a given perimeter (L125); performing actions by instruction (L126); logic problems and puzzles (L127); independent work №15 (L128). *"What did I learn in Grade 2?" (L129–136):* numbers and quantities; elements of algebra; elements of geometry; logical reasoning; mathematical modelling; review; reference material p.137; glossary p.139.
**Honest negatives (Ospanov G2 set):** no area (perimeter only, despite Akpaeva's Part 4 heading mentioning area, Ospanov does not claim it); no fractions; no division with remainder; tables only for 2–5; no three-digit arithmetic beyond round hundreds.

### Cross-series comparison (for the merge step, brief)

- **Three parallel Grade 1–2 systems** are held: Almatykitap (Akpaeva), NIS (Zhakupova), Atamura (Ospanov). All three cap Grade 1 arithmetic at 20 (plus whole tens) and Grade 2 at 100 (plus round hundreds to 1000).
- **Multiplication/division enter in Grade 2 in all three**, always as repeated addition / equal sharing with tables for 2–5 only.
- **Equations appear in Grade 1** in Akpaeva (Part 2) and Zhakupova (Part 3) and are practised from the first Grade 2 lessons in all three series; Ospanov defers formal equations to Grade 2 Part 2 but uses unknown-number boxes in Grade 2 Part 1.
- **Sets, ∈/∉, true/false statements, "three-at-a-time" combinations** appear in all three Grade 2 series — a distinctive shared strand of the renewed Kazakh programme.
- **Perimeter** is Grade 2 Part-final material in Zhakupova and Ospanov (with formulas); Akpaeva's Part 4 teaches perimeter under a section heading that also names area, but sets no area lessons.
- **Money** (tenge coins/notes, price, change) sits in Grade 1 Part 2/Part 4 (Akpaeva/Zhakupova) and inside the Grade 2 multiplication units (Zhakupova Pt3, Ospanov Pt2).

## Kazakhstan Maths — Band B (Grades 3–4) — Reading Draft

Band: 62 files, 7,898 PDF pages (per band-lists.txt). All files are Алматыкітап (Almatykitap) publications, series authors А.Б. Акпаева (A.B. Akpaeva), Л.А. Лебедева (L.A. Lebedeva), М.Ж. Мынжасарова (M.Zh. Mynzhasarova), Т.В. Лихобабенко (T.V. Likhobabenko) — workbooks carry a shorter author list (see entries). All student books are image-only scans (okulyk.kz / okulyk.com watermarks) except front/back matter text layers on the 12xx scans; the two Grade 4 teacher books are born-digital full text.

### Clustering rule applied

(a) Re-scans / different print-files of the same edition + language + part = ONE work (verified by imprint + TOC + interior spot pages, not covers alone).
(b) Kazakh vs Russian (and, as found here, Uyghur) editions of the same series + part = same work in several language editions: maths content filed ONCE per part, all language editions listed, with cross-language spot-checks.
(c) Different years/editions = distinct works, filed separately. A pure reprint (new set ISBN, same part ISBNs, same pagination, © unchanged) is filed as a printing of the same edition, not a new work.
(d) Workbooks and teacher books = separate components, each its own entry with a companion verdict.

## GRADE 3 — STUDENT'S BOOKS

Series design (all four parts, all languages): the year is one continuous numbered course, Lessons 1–170, grouped in sections (1A/1B/1C … 4C) under cross-curricular themes. Every lesson follows the same frame: lesson goal in a "Ты узнаешь / Ты будешь…" box, numbered tasks carrying icons (lesson goal, "what you will learn", remember, research, group work, pair work, practical work, independent work, homework, creative work, "mathematics in life"), speech-bubble self-talk scripts for algorithms ("Пишу… / Складываю единицы… / Читаю ответ"), and a closing "Составь вопросы и задания по теме урока" prompt. Each part ends with "Проверь себя" self-check spreads organised as АНАЛИЗ / СИНТЕЗ / ОЦЕНКА tasks. Page numbers below are PRINTED pages; in the okulyk.kz scans (85x files) PDF page = printed +1; in the 12xx scans PDF page = printed page for the TOC pages and +0/−1 elsewhere (front matter differs) — printed folios were read off the pages. Cross-language check: KZ and Uyghur TOCs list the identical lessons at identical printed pages as RU; lesson content is the same course translated (spot-checked lessons 9, 41, 82–84, 103–104 in RU against KZ TOC/self-check pages, which reproduce the same tasks and numbers).

### G3-S1 — Grade 3, Part 1 (Lessons 1–40)

Bibliographic: see clustering table (KZ 2018 / KZ 2023 repr.; RU 2019 / RU 2023 repr.; Uyghur 2018). 108 printed pp. Theme: ЖИВАЯ ПРИРОДА (Living Nature); closing theme ЧТО ТАКОЕ ХОРОШО, ЧТО ТАКОЕ ПЛОХО.

Content in teaching order:
- **Section 1A. Numbers within 1000. Addition and subtraction.** L1 Numbers within 1000 (p.4); L2 Sum of place-value addends (p.7); L3–5 Oral addition/subtraction of three-digit numbers (pp.10, 12, 14); L6 Counting in thousands to 1,000,000; sequence of numbers (p.17); L7 Counting in thousands to 1,000,000; sequence of numbers (p.20); L8 Models of multi-digit numbers (p.22); L9–12 The written (column) algorithm for adding/subtracting three-digit numbers, built up over four lessons (pp.24, 27, 29, 31); L13 Consolidation (p.34); L14 Generalisation (p.36); L15 Check yourself (p.39).
- **Section 1B. Multiplication and division.** L16 The property of 1 in multiplication and division (p.41); L17 The property of 0 in multiplication and division; impossibility of dividing by zero (p.44); L18 Actions with 0 and 1; evaluating expressions with brackets (p.46); L19 Modelling problems (p.48); L20 Modelling problems, continued (p.50); L21–23 Area. Units of area (pp.52, 56, 58); L24 Dependence between quantities (p.60).
- **Section 1C. Table multiplication and division.** L25–26 Union and intersection of two sets (pp.63, 67); L27–28 Tree of possibilities (pp.70, 73); L29 Generalisation (p.77); L30 Check yourself (p.80); L31 Multiplication/division table for 6; problems in indirect form (p.82); L32 Formulas (p.85); L33 Table for 7 (p.88); L34 Consolidation (p.91); L35 Tables for 8 and 9 (p.93); L36 Consolidation of the multiplication table; problems with indirect questions (p.95); L37 Solving problems (p.97); L38 Generalisation (p.100); L39–40 Check yourself (p.103).

Methods/examples/problems (from Lessons 1–15 read closely, L9 spread read in full): place-value composition is taught through digit-box models (hundreds/tens/units trays) before the column algorithm; the algorithm is introduced with handwritten column workings on squared paper plus a verbal script the pupil repeats; worked examples include adding/subtracting measures (e.g. metres/decimetres/centimetres converted to a single unit first). Problem types: computations with explanation and checking by the inverse operation, "choose three-digit numbers to make a true equality", word problems in 2–3 steps (mountain heights, distances on a map), compose-your-own-problem tasks. Honest negatives: multiplication/division tables for 2–5 are assumed from Grade 2 (only 6, 7, 8, 9 are taught here); no fractions in Part 1 (only "доля" preparation through area/sets); no formal equation-solving (unknowns appear as boxes).

### G3-S2 — Grade 3, Part 2 (Lessons 41–80)

96 printed pp. Theme: ВРЕМЯ (Time); sections under АРХИТЕКТУРА (Architecture).

- **Section 2A. Shares (доли).** L41 The notion of a share (p.4); L42 Share and fraction (p.7); L43 Reading, writing and comparing fractions (p.9); L44–46 Finding a part of a number and the number from its part (pp.11, 13, 16); L47 Square of a number (p.18); L48 Cube of a number (p.20); L49 Square and cube of a number (p.22); L50–51 Problems on dependence between quantities (pp.24, 26); L52 Check yourself (p.28).
- **Section 2B. Area. Quantities.** L53 Circumference/circle and their elements (p.30); L54 Symmetric and non-symmetric flat figures (p.33); L55–56 Area of rectangle and square (pp.35, 37); L57 Area of a right-angled triangle (p.39); L58 Constructing figures (p.41); L59 Constructing figures (p.43); L60 Solving problems; actions with quantities (p.46).
- **Architecture lessons.** L61 Kilometre. Millimetre (p.48); L62 Tonne. Gram (p.52); L63 Coordinates of points and direction of movement (p.54); L64 Solving problems (p.57); L65 Check yourself (p.59).
- **Section 2C. Oral multiplication and division.** L66 Multiplication/division by 10 and 100 (p.61); L67 Oral ×/÷ of round tens and hundreds (p.64); L68 Oral ×/÷ within 1000 (p.67); L69 Oral ×/÷ of round numbers (p.69); L70 Distributive property of multiplication (p.71); L71 Distributive property of multiplication; problem solving (p.73); L72–73 Techniques for 32·2, 320·2, 32·20 type cases (pp.75, 77); L74 Dividing a sum by a number (p.79); L75 Techniques for division 48:2, 480:2, 480:20 (p.82); L76–78 Multiplication/division techniques (pp.85, 87, 89); L79–80 Check yourself (p.92).

Methods (Lesson 41 read in full): a share is introduced on clock faces — the dial split into equal parts motivates 1/2, 1/3, 1/4 and the fraction notation with the division bar explained in words; comparison of shares is done by folding paper circles/squares. Later lessons move to the standard rules for finding a part of a quantity and the whole from a part, always with picture/strip models first. Squares/cubes of numbers are introduced as repeated multiplication in the same section. Problem types: time-interval problems, unit-conversion problems, area from grids then by formula, coordinate-grid movement tasks.

### G3-S3 — Grade 3, Part 3 (Lessons 81–130)

108 printed pp. Theme: ИСКУССТВО (Art); closing theme ВЫДАЮЩИЕСЯ ЛИЧНОСТИ (Outstanding Personalities).

- **Section 3A. Non-table multiplication and division.** L81 Rational calculation methods (p.4); L82 Using division properties to rationalise calculations (p.6); L83–84 Division with remainder by a one-digit number (pp.8, 10); L85 Non-table multiplication of the type 17·5 and division 84:4 (p.12); L86 Non-table division of the type 96:6 (p.14); L87 Non-table division of the type 75:15 (p.17); L88 Consolidation (p.20); L89–91 Equations of complex structure (pp.22, 24, 26); L92–94 Calculations. Generalisation (pp.28, 30, 32); L95 Check yourself (p.34).
- **Section 3B. Solid figures.** L96 Cube (p.36); L97–100 Cube and rectangular parallelepiped (pp.38, 40, 43, 45); L101 Check yourself (p.47).
- **Section 3C. Written multiplication and division.** L102–103 Written multiplication of a two-digit number by a one-digit number (pp.49, 51); L104 Written multiplication of a three-digit number by a one-digit number without carrying (p.53); L105 The same, general case (p.55); L106 Written division of a two-digit number by a one-digit number without a "crossing" (p.57); L107 The same, general case (p.59); L108–109 Written division of a three-digit number by a one-digit number, staged (pp.61, 63); L110–112 Written division with remainder (pp.65, 67, 69); L113–116 The written ×/÷ algorithm with one carry/crossing (pp.71, 73, 75, 77); L117–121 The algorithm with two crossings (pp.79, 81, 83, 86, 88); L122–127 Applying the written ×/÷ algorithm, incl. consolidation (pp.90, 92, 94, 96, 98, 100); L128 Generalisation (p.102); L129–130 Check yourself (p.104).

Methods (Lessons 82–84, 103–104 read in full): division with remainder is built from sharing objects (beads into boxes), the check "quotient × divisor + remainder" is boxed as a rule and the remainder < divisor condition is memorised ("Запомни!"); the famous-persons spreads tie each algorithm to a Kazakh historical figure (Kasym Khan, Ch. Valikhanov, Y. Altynsarin, Archimedes for division). Written algorithms again use the first-person verbal script next to handwritten column work. Problem types: multi-step composite problems, problems from tables (price/quantity/cost), equations with an unknown component, "compose a problem from a scheme" tasks. **Defect — file 858 (RU 2019): printed pp. 3–4 (the "Дорогой друг!" letter and the first page of Lesson 81) are missing from the scan; from printed p.5 onward the sequence is complete through the contents and colophon (folio-verified at printed pp. 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 31, 51, 61, 91, 105–107); the back cover is also not scanned. The complete RU text of pp. 3–4 is held in files 1223 (2023 reprint) and the content in all other language files.** One deliberate teaching example on printed p.10 shows 5 : 6 = 0 (ост. 5) with its check — intentional, not a misprint.

### G3-S4 — Grade 3, Part 4 (Lessons 131–170)

100 printed pp. Themes: ВОДА – ИСТОЧНИК ЖИЗНИ (Water — Source of Life); КУЛЬТУРА ОТДЫХА. ПРАЗДНИКИ (Leisure Culture. Holidays).

- **Section 4A. Written multiplication and division (continued).** L131 Written ×/÷ of the type 140·2, 280:2 (p.4); L132 Consolidation (p.7); L133 Column ×/÷ of the type 102·3, 306:3 (p.9); L134 Consolidation (p.12); L135 Written ×/÷ 104·4, 416:4 (p.14); L136 Consolidation (p.17); L137 Column ×/÷ 134·5, 670:5 (p.20); L138 Consolidation (p.23); L139 Check yourself (p.25).
- **Section 4B. Ways of solving problems.** L140–141 The banknote as paper money (pp.27, 30); L142–143 Subset (pp.33, 36).
- **Leisure theme lessons.** L144 True and false statements (p.39); L145 Solving problems (p.42); L146–147 Problems in 2–3 actions (pp.44, 47); L148–149 Solving problems (pp.49, 51); L150 Consolidation (p.53); L151 Problems with indirect questions (p.55); L152 Solving problems with indirect questions (p.57); L153–154 Problems in 3 actions (pp.59, 61); L155 Solving problems with letter expressions (p.63); L156 Composite problems (p.65); L157 Solving problems by means of an equation (p.67); L158 Consolidation (p.69); L159 Check yourself (p.71).
- **Section 4C. Time.** L160–161 Second (pp.73, 76); L162 Century (p.78); L163 Consolidation (p.81); L164 Measuring units of time (p.83); L165 Telling the time from clocks (p.86); L166 Consolidation (p.88); L167–168 Generalisation (pp.90, 92); L169–170 Check yourself (p.94); closing feature "Учимся с интересом" (p.96).

Methods: the year closes by systematising problem-solving (arithmetic vs algebraic/equation methods are contrasted in L155–157), money work uses real tenge banknotes, and the time section formalises second/century and clock-reading to the minute. Honest negatives for the whole G3 course: no decimal fractions; no negative numbers; percentages are NOT taught in Grade 3 (the word appears nowhere in the TOCs — it arrives in Grade 4 Part 3); division is only by one-digit divisors in written form (two-digit divisors arrive in Grade 4).

## GRADE 3 — WORKBOOKS

### G3-W1…W4 — Рабочая тетрадь / Жұмыс дәптері №1–№4 (companion component)

Bibliographic: Алматыкітап баспасы, 2018. KZ authors Акпаева, Лебедева, Мынжасарова; RU authors Акпаева, Лебедева (title pages). RU №1 verso (file 868, PDF p.4): "Математика. Рабочая тетрадь №1 для учащихся 3 класса общеобразовательной школы / А.Б. Акпаева, Л.А. Лебедева. – Алматы: Алматыкітап баспасы, 2018. – 72 с.; ил. ISBN 978-601-01-3722-6." Printed extent 72 pp each for №1–№3 (76 PDF pp incl. covers) and 68 pp for №4 (72 PDF pp). All four RU numbers print the same verso ISBN 978-601-01-3722-6 and all four KZ numbers the same verso ISBN 978-601-01-3694-6 (shared-ISBN quirk, verified on every verso and on the №1 back-cover barcodes).

Structure and companion verdict: each workbook is keyed lesson-by-lesson to the student's book part of the same number (№1 ↔ Part 1, Lessons 1–40; №2 ↔ Part 2; №3 ↔ Part 3; №4 ↔ Part 4), in the same teaching order — **match confirmed** (№1 RU opens with the Part 1 number work; its front matter sets the self-assessment system: a 5-circle "дорожка успеха" — 1 "Не знаю", 2 "Знаю", 3 "Понимаю", 4 "Понимаю и могу применить", 5 "Могу научить другого" — and "критерии оценивания" descriptors to be filled per lesson). Tasks are write-in versions of the textbook's task types: complete the sequence/pattern, spot the wrong number in a sequence, compute and check, fill tables, draw and measure. The workbooks add no new theory: they are practice + self-assessment companions, and the KZ and RU numbers are the same tasks in the two languages. Defects: none seen; scans are clean okulyk.kz copies including covers.


## GRADE 4 — STUDENT'S BOOKS, 2019 EDITION

Series design: again one continuous course, Lessons 1–170 in four parts, but the frame changes: a mascot book-character (Алматыкітап) opens and closes each lesson; lessons are typed (ИЗУЧЕНИЕ НОВОГО / ЗАКРЕПЛЕНИЕ / ОБОБЩЕНИЕ / КОНТРОЛЬ — stated on the "Как пользоваться учебником" spread, printed p.2 of Parts 3–4); tasks are grouped under named rubrics (РАБОТА В ПАРЕ, РАБОТА В ГРУППЕ, ТВОРЧЕСКАЯ РАБОТА, ТЫ ИССЛЕДОВАТЕЛЬ, МАТЕМАТИКА В ЖИЗНИ, ДОМАШНЯЯ РАБОТА, ЗАПОМНИ!). Each part ends with a **Глоссарий** (Glossary) of the part's terms with short definitions — a real content component, quoted in the entries where it matters. Companion characters: Арман and Света (KZ: Арман және Сәуле; Uyghur: Арман вә Алийәм). Page numbers are PRINTED pages (folio = PDF −1 in the okulyk.kz scans 81–84/103–106; the 12xx scans differ in front matter only). KZ and Uyghur TOCs match the RU lessons and pages exactly (verified for all parts in KZ; for Uyghur in Parts 1 and by imprint/spot pages in Parts 2–4).

### G4-S1 — Grade 4, Part 1, 2019 edition (Lessons 1–40)

148 printed pp. Themes: «Моя Родина – Казахстан!», «Человеческие ценности / Ценности».

- **Section 1A. Numbering of multi-digit numbers and operations with them** (p.6). L1–2 Review of Grade 3 (pp.7, 11); L3 Formation of multi-digit numbers; the natural series; composition of multi-digit numbers; sequences (p.14); L4 The millions class; rounding numbers (p.18); L5–6 Arithmetic operations with multi-digit numbers (pp.22, 26); L7–8 Written addition/subtraction of multi-digit numbers (pp.29, 33); L9 Milligram (p.36); L10 Solid geometric figures; measuring volume (p.40); L11 Are and hectare — units of area (p.44); L12 Converting units of measure and operating with them (p.48); L13 Fractions of units of time (p.52); L14 Euro (€), dollar ($): operations with currencies (p.56); L15 Rouble (₽): operations with currencies (p.60); L16 Generalisation (p.63); L17 Check yourself (p.66).
- **Section 1B. Multiplication and division by a one-digit number** (p.68). L18 Oral calculation techniques with multi-digit numbers (p.69); L19–20 Multiplying/dividing a multi-digit number by a one-digit number (pp.73, 77); L21–22 Divisibility tests for 2, 5, 10 (pp.81, 85); L23 Multiplying/dividing by 10, 100, 1000 (p.89); L24 Division with remainder by 10, 100, 1000 (p.92); L25 Relations between sets (p.95); L26 Consolidation (p.99); L27 Check yourself (p.102).
- **Section 1C. Speed, time, distance** (p.104). L28–29 Motion problems (pp.105, 108); L30 Consolidation and generalisation (p.111); L31 Commutative/associative properties of set union and intersection in problem solving (p.114); L32 Consolidation (p.118); L33 Review of the section (p.121); L34 Check yourself (p.124).
- **Section 1D. Geometric figures** (p.126). L35 Cube and rectangular parallelepiped; volume of the rectangular parallelepiped (p.127); L36 Dependence between quantities in problems (p.130); L37 Area of combined figures (p.134); L38 Generalisation (p.137); L39–40 Check yourself (p.140). Glossary p.142.

Methods (Lessons 28–29 read in full): motion is taught through the S = v·t "formula triangle", timeline diagrams marked hour by hour, and tables (speed/time/distance with one unknown); every lesson mixes rubric-tagged tasks, including research and "compose your own problem" closers. The Part 1 glossary defines the part's own terms (among them ар, гектар, диаграмма Эйлера-Венна, множество, объединение и пересечение множеств, скорость) in words of the book's own level; the work/motion glossary terms производительность, совместная работа, скорость сближения/удаления and the division-with-remainder check formula appear in the Part 2 glossary (p.142), not in Part 1's. Honest negatives: numbers are worked with up to 1,000,000 (the teacher guide notes billions are mentioned but not used in tasks); no written division by two-digit numbers yet (Part 2); currencies are limited to tenge, euro, dollar, rouble conversions at whole-number rates.

### G4-S2 — Grade 4, Part 2, 2019 edition (Lessons 41–80)

144 printed pp. Themes: «Культурное наследие», «Мир профессий».

- **Section 2A. Multiplication and division** (p.6). L41 Multiplying/dividing a number by a product (p.7); L42 Oral techniques for numbers ending in zeros (p.11); L43 Written multiplication of multi-digit numbers ending in zeros (p.15); L44 Written division of numbers ending in zeros (p.18); L45 Consolidation (p.21); L46 Written multiplication by a two-digit number (p.25); L47 Written division of a multi-digit number by a two-digit number (p.29); L48 Division by a two-digit number with remainder (p.33); L49 Written division by a two-digit number (p.36); L50 Written division by a two-digit number with a zero in the quotient (p.39); L51–52 Written division by a two-digit number / written division of multi-digit numbers (pp.42, 45); L53 Algorithm of multiplication by a three-digit number (p.48); L54 Algorithm of division by a three-digit number (p.52); L55 Multiplication and division algorithms for a three-digit number (p.56); L56–58 Algorithm of division by a three-digit number (pp.59, 62, 65); L59 Multiplication and division algorithms for a three-digit number (p.69); L60 Generalisation (p.72); L61 Check yourself (p.74).
- **Section 2B. Solving problems** (p.76). L62–63 Productivity (pp.77, 81); L64 Productivity; joint work (p.85); L65 Joint work (p.88); L66–67 Proportional division problems (pp.91, 94); L68–70 Finding an unknown from two differences (pp.98, 102, 106); L71–73 Meeting-motion problems (pp.110, 114, 118); L74–76 Motion in opposite directions (pp.121, 125, 128); L77 Motion problems (p.132); L78 Check yourself (p.136); L79–80 Generalisation (p.138). Glossary p.142.

Methods: this is the algorithmic core of Grade 4 — written ×/÷ is extended stepwise (zeros → two-digit → three-digit), each algorithm stated as a numbered procedure and practised first on round numbers; the problem section then formalises the three great quantity-triples of the course (work: productivity/time/amount; motion: speed/time/distance; and proportional division), with formula language carried from the glossaries (the productivity / joint-work / approach-and-separation-speed terms and the remainder check formula a = b·c + r, r < b are defined in this part's own glossary, p.142). (TOC titles and pages above are as printed in the Part 2 contents; the 2019 and 2020 TOCs agree with each other.)

### G4-S3 — Grade 4, Part 3, 2019 edition (Lessons 81–130)

160 printed pp. Themes: «Природные явления», «Охрана окружающей среды».

- **Section 3A. Motion and yield problems** (p.4). L81 Problems on dependence between quantities; pie chart (круговая диаграмма) (p.5); L82–83 Yield (урожайность) (pp.9, 12); L84 Graphs (p.15); L85 Solving problems (p.19); L86–87 Catch-up motion (движение вдогонку) (pp.22, 25); L88 Solving catch-up motion problems; motion graphs (p.29); L89–90 Motion with lagging behind (движение с отставанием) (pp.32, 35); L91 Catch-up and lagging motion (p.39); L92 Comparing catch-up/lagging problems; truth and falsity (p.43); L93–94 Statements; truth and falsity (pp.46, 49); L95 Generalisation: statements (p.53); L96–99 Logic problems (pp.56, 59, 63, 66); L100–102 Combinatorial problems by the enumeration method (pp.69, 72, 75); L103–104 Generalisation (pp.78, 81); L105 Check yourself (p.84).
- **Section 3B. Fractions and percents. Problems** (p.86). L106 Percents (p.87); L107 Percents (p.90); L108 Percents, consolidation (p.93); L109 Fractions; comparing fractions (p.96); L110–111 Adding/subtracting fractions (pp.100, 103); L112 Proper and improper fractions (p.106); L113 Fractions; mixed numbers (p.110); L114 Fractions, consolidation (p.113); L115 Problems on finding a part of the whole (p.116); L116 Problems on finding the whole from its part (p.119); L117 Composing and solving problems (p.122); L118 Consolidation; solving problems (p.125); L119–120 Generalisation (pp.128, 131); L121 Check yourself (p.134); L122 Generalisation (p.136).
- **Section 3C. Circle** (p.138). L123–125 Circle and disc (окружность и круг) (pp.139, 143, 146); L126 Generalisation (p.149); L127 Check yourself (p.152); L128–129 Generalisation (p.154); L130 Generalisation (p.156). Glossary p.158.

Methods (Lessons 106 and 112–113 read in full): percent is defined exactly as a named hundredth — the "Запомни!" box states that the share 1/100 is called a percent and 1/100 = 1%; finding 1% by dividing the whole into 100 equal parts and p% as 1% × p is worked on money, mass and length; pie charts/diagrams are read and re-expressed as percents. Fraction work stays with like denominators for +/− (the glossary defines proper/improper fractions, mixed numbers, numerator/denominator); comparing uses same-denominator and same-numerator cases. Honest negatives: no percent increase/decrease or interest; no unlike-denominator addition (that is Grade 5); probability is present only as combinatorics-by-enumeration, with no probability numbers.

### G4-S4 — Grade 4, Part 4, 2019 edition (Lessons 131–170)

144 printed pp. Themes: «Путешествие в космос», «Путешествие в будущее». Title pages of the 2019 KZ/RU printings call Parts 3–4 a «Проект для апробации / Апробацияға арналған жоба» (pilot-approval draft) — as printed.

- **Section 4A. Double inequalities, equations, expressions** (p.4). L131 Double inequalities (p.5); L132 Solving double inequalities (p.9); L133 Double inequalities (p.13); L134 Equations (p.16); L135 Numerical and letter expressions; transforming expressions (p.20); L136 Equations solved by transformation (p.24); L137 Numerical and letter expressions in problems (p.28); L138 Composing letter expressions from problems (p.32); L139–140 Comparing expressions containing fractions with the same denominators (pp.36, 39); L141 Generalisation (p.43); L142 Check yourself (p.46); L143 Check yourself (p.50).
- **Section 4B. Problems** (p.52). L144–145 Rational methods of calculation and problem solving (pp.53, 57); L146 Solving problems (p.61); L147–148 Problems on finding the edge length and volume of a rectangular parallelepiped (pp.65, 69); L149–152 Composite problems (pp.73, 77, 80, 83); L153 Check yourself (p.86).
- **Section 4C. Geometric figures** (p.88). L154 Triangles (p.89); L155 Measuring angles (p.93); L156 Constructing angles (p.97); L157 Measuring and constructing angles (p.101); L158 Area of combined figures (p.105); L159–160 Symmetric figures (pp.108, 112); L161–162 Nets (развёртки) (pp.115, 119); L163–164 Sequences (pp.122, 125); L165–166 Generalisation (pp.128, 131); L167 Check yourself (p.134); L168–170 Final generalisation (pp.136, 139). Glossary p.142.

Methods (Lessons 131–132 read in full): a double inequality is solved as the intersection of two solution sets on the number ray — the "Запомни!" box states explicitly that the solution of a double inequality can be the intersection or union of two sets, linking back to the Grade 3/Part 1 set work; angles are measured and constructed with the protractor (транспортир, in the glossary); the course closes by explicitly preparing the move to Grade 5. Honest negatives for Grade 4 as a whole: no decimals anywhere; no negative numbers; no coordinates beyond Grade 3's point/direction work; algebra is limited to equations with one unknown solved by transformation and to letter expressions — no systems, no graphs of functions (motion graphs are read, not plotted from formulas).
