### WORK 5 — Electronic supplement to the Mektep Grade 6 *Mathematics* (teacher component) (Mektep, 2018)

**As printed (Kazakh edition):** А. Е. Әбілқасымова, Т. П. Кучер, З. Ә. Жұмағұлова,
*Математика. 6-сынып. Электронды қосымша* — "Жалпы білім беретін мектептің
6-сынып мұғалімдеріне арналған құрал" (a manual for teachers of Grade 6 of the
general-education school), Almaty: Mektep, 2018, 111 pp. **As printed (Russian
edition):** А. Е. Абылкасымова, Т. П. Кучер — only these two names appear on the
Russian cover, in its bibliographic entry and in its © line — *Математика.
6 класс. Электронное приложение. Пособие для учителей 6 классов
общеобразовательных школ*, Almaty: Mektep, 2018, 109 pp. **No ISBN is printed
in either edition** (neither imprint carries an ISBN, a print run or a ministry
approval line — the p. 2 imprint of each is only the bibliographic description
plus the © notice; searched in the full text of both files). Held files:
grade-06…student_1558.pdf (Kazakh, 111 PDF pp) and grade-06…teacher_1557.pdf
(Russian, 109 PDF pp). In both files printed page = PDF page throughout
(verified at the cover, contents, mid-book cards and the final test pages).
**Record correction, confirmed from the books themselves:** file 1558 is
recorded in the library as a student's book — that is wrong. Its own cover
titles it the *Elektron dy qosymsha* "for teachers of Grade 6", its
introduction addresses teachers working with the Mektep textbook, and nothing
in it is addressed to pupils as a textbook. It is the Kazakh edition of the
same teacher supplement as file 1557, and the pair is filed here as one work
in two language editions. Unlike the other Band C PDFs, both files carry a
real, usable text layer (an OKULYK distributor's header/footer stamp sits on
every held page, outside the book's own pagination); the full Kazakh text
layer was read end to end and checked against rendered pages at the start,
middle and end, and the Russian file was checked at cover, imprint, contents
and spot interiors throughout.

**What the book says it is (Introduction, p. 3 in both editions).** The
supplement is a component part of the teaching–methodical complex (KZ: of the
methodological manual) for the Mektep Grade 6 *Mathematics* textbook of
Abylkasymova, Kucher and Zhumagulova — both introductions name all three as
the *textbook's* authors, whatever each cover says about the supplement
itself. For every topic it offers three things: oral-work tasks,
critical-thinking / individual-and-group-work tasks, and formative-assessment
tests. The introduction is the book's only expository text, and it is
addressed to teachers, not pupils (see the companion verdict below).

#### Structure (page numbers are the printed pages; KZ and RU spans given)

**1. Introduction (p. 3, both editions)** — purpose and instructions for the
three components: oral tasks open the lesson and are meant for display (e.g.
on an electronic board); the cards are printable and may be used at any stage
of the lesson; the tests are built on V. S. Avanesov's test-construction
theory, deliberately short (5, 6 or 7 items) but covering the whole of the
corresponding §, and are run *before* the class moves to the next topic,
because a pupil is taken to be ready for new material only after mastering at
least ~70% of the previous: the level is reached with 4 correct out of 5, 5
out of 6, and — KZ wording — 6 out of 7 (the RU introduction differs here;
see KZ/RU differences). **Contents (p. 4, both editions)** lists only these
four blocks; the book has no chapter dividers of its own.

**2. Oral work — tasks for oral work / mathematical warm-up (KZ pp. 5–21;
RU pp. 5–21).** One set for **every one of the textbook's §§1–55, in order**
(55/55 in both editions; KZ starts: §§1–4 p. 5, §§5–7 p. 6, §§8–10 p. 7,
§§11–14 p. 8, §§15–17 p. 9, §§18–20 p. 10, §§21–23 p. 11, §§24–26 p. 12,
§§27–29 p. 13, §§30–32 p. 14, §§33–35 p. 15, §§36–38 p. 16, §§39–42 p. 17,
§§43–46 p. 18, §§47–49 p. 19, §§50–52 p. 20, §§53–55 p. 21; the RU sets sit
on the same pages with a drift of at most one page, e.g. §46 RU p. 19 vs KZ
p. 18). Every set has the same built-in mechanic: a grid of short computations
or one-line problems, each task tagged with a letter and matched against a row
of ready answers; placing the letters over their answers assembles a hidden
word ("сөз / Слово") tied to the lesson's theme, so the set is self-checking.
The heading of each set is the *new* §'s number and title, but the tasks
themselves deliberately consolidate the just-studied prerequisite material —
e.g. the §7 set (positive/negative numbers) is built from circumference→area
calculations of §6, the §6 set from map-scale work of §5, the §5 set from the
inverse-proportionality coefficient of §3 — exactly as the introduction
describes: oral work rehearses what the new topic stands on.

**3. Critical-thinking cards (KZ pp. 22–84; RU pp. 22–80).** One task set for
**53 of the 55 §§ — every § except §31 and §55** (KZ starts: §1 p. 22,
§§2–3 p. 23, §4 p. 24, §5 p. 26, §6 p. 27, §7 p. 28, §§8–9 p. 29, §10 p. 31,
§§11–12 p. 32, §13 p. 34, §14 p. 36, §15 p. 37, §16 p. 38, §17 p. 39,
§18 p. 41, §§19–20 p. 43, §§21–22 p. 44, §23 pp. 45–46, §24 p. 47, §25 p. 48,
§26 p. 49, §27 p. 50, §§28–29 p. 51, §30 p. 53, §32 p. 54, §33 p. 55, §34
p. 56, §35 p. 58, §36 p. 59, §37 p. 61, §38 p. 62, §39 p. 63, §40 p. 64,
§41 p. 65, §42 p. 66, §§43–44 p. 67, §45 p. 68, §46 p. 69, §47 p. 70, §§48–50
pp. 73–74, §51 p. 77, §52 p. 78, §53 p. 79, §54 p. 83; RU starts drift
progressively earlier, §53 at p. 75, §54 at p. 79, section ending p. 80). The
two missing §§ are precisely the textbook's two "solve text problems"
capstones — §31 (by equations) and §55 (by systems) — and the RU introduction
itself says a card set was prepared for every topic *except two*, which
matches. Formats used, in the book's own repertoire of techniques (KZ intro:
"Сенемін-сенбеймін", "Келісемін-келіспеймін", "Дейін-Кейін", "Ирек",
"Айналмалы стансы", "Қарлы кесек", "Пазл", etc.; the RU intro's list is
longer — see differences):
- *Numbered cards* (парақша / Карточка 1–4 for many §§; 67 numbered-card
  headers in each edition): two-column tables — a sequenced prompt chain in
  the left column, an "Answers" (Жауабы / Ответы) column on the right, mostly
  blank ruled space to complete, with the key naming answer occasionally
  printed in it (e.g. §53, card 1, where the expected answer to "what is this
  method called?" is pre-printed). The chain typically walks through one
  worked solution step by step — transform, ask whether the result is
  equivalent, solve, find the second unknown, write the solution, name the
  method — and then repeats the pattern on further examples (§§53–54 cards
  are the clearest case, 4 cards each).
- *Believe / Don't believe* and *Agree / Disagree* statement tables with a
  "Conclusion" column (§1, §32 and others): numbered assertions about the
  §'s content to be marked +/– before and resolved after study.
- *Before–After* ("Дейін–Кейін" / "До–После") tables (§24, §38): the pupil's
  first answer, revised answer, and a "I was right (not right), because…"
  column.
- *INSERT marking grids* (the "V / + / – / ?" legend — knew it / new /
  thought otherwise / didn't understand) for §§28 and 30: for §30 the whole
  supplement entry is just the blank INSERT grid printed under the § heading
  (KZ p. 53) — the marking is done on the textbook's own text.
Most cards do **not** name the technique they realise; the format is the only
indication. The §23 cards (distributive property, expanding brackets) sit on
the image-only pages (see defects): four cards, each asking pupils to
evaluate an expression in both its bracketed and expanded forms, judge
whether the two equalities hold, and state the transformation for
a·(b + c) and (a + b)·c in letters.

**4. Formative-assessment tests (KZ pp. 85–111; RU pp. 81–109).** One test
for **each of §§1–54 — there is no test for §55** (KZ starts: §1 p. 85,
§§2–3 p. 86, §§4–5 p. 87, §§6–7 p. 88, §§8–9 p. 89, §§10–11 p. 90,
§§12–14 p. 91, §§15–17 p. 92, §§18–19 p. 93, §§20–21 p. 94, §§22–23 p. 95,
§§24–26 p. 96, §§27–28 p. 97, §§29–31 p. 98, §32 p. 99, §33 p. 100, §34
p. 101, §§35–36 p. 102, §37 p. 103, §§38–39 p. 104, §§40–43 pp. 105–106,
§§44–46 pp. 106–107, §§47–48 p. 108, §§49–50 p. 109, §§51–53 p. 110, §54
p. 111; RU starts run 4 pages earlier, §44 test at p. 104, §§53–54 at
p. 109). Item types, mixed within each test under printed instruction lines:
choose/mark the correct answer (multiple choice — 5 options in the early
tests, 2–3 later), complete the statement (fill the blank in a definition,
a computation or a system's solution), establish the correct sequence
(ordering the steps of an algorithm, e.g. §53), and picture-choice items
(§44: pick the correct drawing of a cuboid / ball / parallelepiped from four
figures). Length as promised in the introduction: 5–7 items (spot-verified,
e.g. the §1 test has 6). **No answers or key to the tests are printed
anywhere in the book**; the volume ends immediately after the §54 test
(KZ p. 111, RU p. 109) — no answers section, no bibliography, no closing
colophon beyond the p. 2 imprint.

#### Companion verdict (in full)

This supplement is the teacher component of **Work 4** (Abylkasymova, Kucher,
Zhumagulova, *Mathematics*, Grade 6, Mektep, 2018) and of no other book: its
introduction names that textbook, and the match is §-for-§ and total. Every
heading in all three components reproduces the textbook's § number **and**
§ title verbatim (in the edition's language), in the textbook's order,
running without a break across the textbook's two parts (Pt 1 §§1–26, Pt 2
§§27–55) and its ten chapters — the supplement simply never prints the
chapter or part divisions. Coverage against the textbook's 55 §§: oral work
**55/55**; critical-thinking cards **53/55** (none for §31 or §55); formative
tests **54/55** (none for §55). What a teacher gets from it, per topic, is a
ready start-of-lesson oral set with a built-in self-check (the decoded word),
a printable card set that stages the topic's core solution or concept as
guided questions with answer space, and a short ready-to-print test with a
stated mastery threshold for deciding whether the class may move on.

**It contains no new student theory — verified against the textbook, not
assumed.** The only continuous prose in the book is the one-page introduction,
addressed to teachers (test theory after Avanesov, the ~70% readiness
criterion, the technique repertoire). Everything after p. 4 is tasks: the
cards *elicit* the textbook's rules as questions (is this equality true? how
can a·(b + c) be rewritten? does the distributive property hold for rational
numbers?) and step through solutions of textbook type (the §21 card, for
instance, lays out worked periodic-decimal computations of the kind the
textbook develops in §§20–21 for pupils to analyse); the tests only ask for
the textbook's definitions, rules and procedures in choose/complete/order
form. No definition, rule, notation or topic appears here that is not in the
same-numbered § of Work 4 (oral sets, by design, recycle *earlier* §§'
material under the new §'s heading — prerequisite revision, not new
content). The genuinely new material is pedagogical apparatus only: the
decoded-word oral mechanic, the critical-thinking card formats, the test
specification and thresholds, and the occasional pre-printed key term in a
card's answer column. It is therefore filed as a separate component of
Work 4 — never as a student's book, and it adds no course content beyond
Work 4; its value to the courses is as a bank of §-keyed warm-ups, guided
cards and formative test items for the same 55 topics.

#### Differences between the Kazakh and Russian files

- **Authorship as printed.** The Kazakh cover, bibliographic entry and ©
  line all name **three** authors (Әбілқасымова, Кучер, Жұмағұлова); the
  Russian cover, entry and © line name **two** (Абылкасымова, Кучер) — the
  Russian edition credits Zhumagulova only inside the introduction, as a
  textbook author. (Surname spelling follows the band's settled pattern:
  Әбілқасымова KZ = Абылкасымова RU.)
- **Page count 111 vs 109 — explained, no content missing.** Front matter is
  identical (cover, imprint, introduction p. 3, contents p. 4) and the oral
  section spans pp. 5–21 in **both**. The cards take 63 pp in KZ (22–84) but
  59 pp in RU (22–80); the tests take 27 pp in KZ (85–111) but 29 pp in RU
  (81–109). Net −4 +2 = the 2-page difference. §-by-§ coverage is identical
  in the two editions (same 55 oral sets, same 53 card topics, same 54
  tests, same task content in translation — the closing §§53–54 tests are
  item-for-item the same); the drift is typesetting and language length
  only. (The held PDFs also differ slightly in page geometry — KZ
  481.89 × 680.315 pts, RU 496.063 × 708.661 pts.)
- **Contents pages differ in form, and the RU one has an error.** The KZ
  contents lists 4 entries (introduction 3, oral 5, cards 22, tests 85) and
  every number checks out. The RU contents lists 5 entries (it adds a
  self-entry "Содержание — 4") and misprints the cards' start as p. 21: the
  oral §55 set in fact ends on p. 21 and the cards' heading stands on
  p. 22. Its other entries (introduction 3, oral 5, tests 81) are correct.
- **The introductions are not literal translations of each other.** The RU
  introduction is the fuller text: it gives the oral section its double name
  ("устная работа, математическая разминка"), states plainly that the oral
  tasks consolidate previously studied material, adds that the tests rest
  also on the author's dissertation research, and lists ten techniques
  (adding "Ромашка Блума", "Фишбоун", "Инсерт" to the KZ list's seven +
  "etc."). One substantive divergence: the mastery threshold for a 7-item
  test is **6 of 7 in the KZ introduction** but **"5 or 6 of 7" in the RU
  introduction** (4/5 and 5/6 agree in both).
- **Image-only pages differ** (see defects): KZ has four (pp. 45, 46, 106,
  107), RU has three (pp. 44, 45, 104) — in RU only the §44 test is
  image-only, while in KZ the tests for §§43 (end)–46 occupy two image
  pages.

#### Honest negatives and defects

- **No ISBN, no print run, no approval line** in either imprint — a thinner
  bibliographic record than the Work 4 textbook itself carries; the edition
  can be cited only by author/title/publisher/year/page count.
- **No test key and no answers section at all**: a teacher must work every
  test personally; only the oral sets self-check (via the decoded word) and
  the cards mostly leave their Answers column blank.
- **The two problem-solving capstones are the least served topics**: §31 and
  §55 have no critical-thinking card, and §55 has no formative test either —
  each gets only its oral warm-up set.
- **Image-only pages inside otherwise text-layer PDFs**: KZ pp. 45–46
  (the whole of the §23 cards) and pp. 106–107 (§43 test end, §§44–46 tests),
  RU pp. 44–45 (§23 cards) and p. 104 (§44 test) carry no text layer at all —
  a text search alone would wrongly conclude that §23 has no cards and (KZ)
  that §§44–46 have no tests; all were verified from rendered pages. A
  smaller text-layer blemish of the same kind: the axis labels of the §49
  test's graph (KZ p. 109) extract as mojibake, though the page itself is
  legible.
- **"Electronic" in name only, as held**: the introduction's use cases are
  projection and printing, and the held PDFs contain no interactive elements
  (no fillable fields were found) — they are ordinary printable PDFs.
- The RU contents misprint (cards at "p. 21" for p. 22) is noted above; the
  KZ contents is accurate. No missing, duplicated or out-of-order pages were
  found in either file; both end complete at their stated page counts.

### Citation check — Band C (2026-11-04)

Independent spot-check of this draft against the held PDFs (checker ≠ reader).
Each anchor was verified on the exact rendered PDF page, using the PDF↔printed
mapping the draft states for that work (Work 4's stated mapping was itself one
of the anchors — see correction 5).

**Totals: 86 anchors checked — 81 PASSED, 5 CORRECTED, 0 NOT FOUND.**

Per work:
- Work 1 — Aldamuratova et al., G5, Atamura 2017 (123, 124, 146, 147): 18 checked, 18 passed, 0 corrected, 0 not found.
- Work 2 — Abylkasymova et al., G5, Mektep 2017 (125, 126, 148, 149, 1392, 1352): 18 checked, 16 passed, 2 corrected, 0 not found.
- Work 3 — Aldamuratova et al., G6, Atamura 2018 (180, 181, 207, 208): 17 checked, 15 passed, 2 corrected, 0 not found.
- Work 4 — Abylkasymova et al., G6, Mektep 2018 (182, 183, 209, 210, 1375, 1445, 1450, 1380): 17 checked, 16 passed, 1 corrected, 0 not found.
- Work 5 — Electronic supplement to Work 4 (1558, 1557): 16 checked, 16 passed, 0 corrected, 0 not found.

#### Corrections made (each fixed in the draft above)

1. **Work 2, file 148 defect wording.** The draft said the Russian Pt 1 PDF's
   extra page comes from "answers spread over two pages". In fact PDF p. 144
   carries the answers tail and the colophon together, and the extra PDF
   p. 145 is a blank leaf (rendered and viewed, files 148 pp. 144–145).
2. **Work 2, Pt 2 headnote (file 126).** The draft said "the PDF's 129th page
   is the colophon leaf". In fact the colophon stands on printed p. 128
   (ИБ № 5608, run 100,000, seen on the rendered page) and PDF p. 129 is a
   blank leaf.
3. **Work 3, Pt 1 colophon dates (file 180, p. 208).** The draft read
   "signed to print 11.01.2018, printed 15.05.2018". The colophon reads
   «Теруге 11.01.2018 ж. берілді» (sent to typesetting 11.01.2018) and
   «Басуға 15.05.2018 ж. қол қойылды» (signed to print 15.05.2018) — the two
   labels were swapped in the draft; corrected.
4. **Work 3, Pt 2 colophon dates (file 181, p. 224).** Same swap: typesetting
   20.01.2018, signed to print 14.05.2018 (draft had the reverse); corrected.
5. **Work 4, Kazakh page mapping (files 182, 183).** The draft stated
   "PDF p. = printed p. + 1 (an un-numbered title leaf follows the cover)".
   The scans in fact run PDF p. = printed p. throughout: in file 182 the
   cover is printed p. 1, §1 stands at PDF 18 = printed 18, the contents at
   PDF 183 = printed 183, and the QR/colophon leaf at PDF 184 = printed 184;
   the 185th PDF page is a blank leaf (same in file 183: §27 at PDF 4 =
   printed 4, PDF 185 blank). Headnote corrected, and the clustering table's
   "185 (184 pp + cover)" entries for files 182 and 183 changed to
   "185 (184 pp + 1 blank scan leaf)".

#### Special claims verified

**(a) Author-spelling settlement — CONFIRMED.** File 125 (Kazakh) imprint
p. 2 gives Әбілқасымова with ISBN 978-601-07-0846-4 (set 978-601-07-0860-0);
file 148 (Russian) imprint p. 2 gives Абылкасымова with ISBN
978-601-07-0847-1 (set 978-601-07-0859-4). The colophons of both editions
give the identical full name Абылкасымова Алма Есимбековна with the same
co-authors (Кучер Татьяна Павловна, Жумагулова Зауре Абылкеновна), same
publisher (Mektep, Almaty) and year (2017). One person, one series: the
draft's settlement stands.

**(b) Work 5 keying counts — CONFIRMED.** Counted from the supplement files'
own text layer, with the image-only pages verified from renders: oral work
covers all §§1–55 in both editions (55/55); critical-thinking cards cover
53/55 — none for §31 or §55 (the §23 cards, invisible to text search, are
present on the image-only pages: KZ pp. 45–46, RU pp. 44–45, rendered and
viewed); formative tests cover 54/55 — none for §55 (the KZ tests for
§§43–46 on image-only pp. 106–107 and the RU §44 test on image-only p. 104
were rendered and viewed; both volumes end immediately after the §54 test,
KZ p. 111 / RU p. 109). **No answer key** or answers section exists anywhere
in either file.

#### Checked and passed (highlights)

Work 1: both KZ imprints and colophons (set/Pt ISBNs, 224/192 pp, runs
50,000/50,000, signed 22.05.2017 / 06.06.2017), RU Pt 1 imprint with the

## Kazakhstan — Maths — Band D-G7: Grade 7 Algebra + Geometry

First read, maths phase. Band = 32 files, 5,434 PDF pages (record count). All 32 PDFs are
image-only scans (the only text layer is the okulyk.com watermark line); every page cited
below was read from rendered pages. Printed page numbers are given; where the PDF page
differs, both are noted. Covers/imprints govern over the record throughout.

### Clustering rule applied

(a) Same edition + language + part in several files = one work. **No true re-scan clusters
were found in this band** — every apparent repeat turned out to be a different language
edition or a different edition year (see below). (b) Different language editions of the same
series + part = one work, content filed once per work, all language editions listed, with
spot-checks across languages. (c) Different years/editions = distinct works. (d) Each file
filed as what its own pages prove it is (textbook / didactic materials / problem collection /
fragment), with record discrepancies stated.

**Result: 32 files → 15 distinct works.** The band's big surprise: Kazakhstan's Grade 7
maths is published not only in Kazakh and Russian but also in **Uyghur and Uzbek** editions
for Uyghur-/Uzbek-medium schools, plus a separate English-medium line (Astana-Kitap) and the
NIS (Nazarbayev Intellectual Schools) integrated *Mathematics* course in Kazakh/Russian/English.

### Clustering table — every file → its work

#### Work 1 — NIS *Mathematics*, Grade 7, Parts 1–2 (NIS Programme, Astana, 2018)
Integrated maths (algebra + geometry + statistics in one course), NOT an "Algebra" textbook.

| algebra_1053 (144p) | Part 1, **Kazakh**, ISBN 978-601-328-224-4 |
| algebra_1054 (144p) | Part 1, **Russian**, ISBN 978-601-328-228-2 |
| algebra_1055 (144p) | Part 1, **English** ("Maths, Grade 7, Part 1"), ISBN 978-601-328-232-9 |
| algebra_1056 (212p) | Part 2, **Kazakh**, ISBN 978-601-328-302-9 |
| algebra_1057 (212p) | Part 2, **Russian**, ISBN 978-601-328-300-5 |
| algebra_1058 (212p) | Part 2, **English**, ISBN 978-601-328-305-0 |

wrong on language for four of six, and Забара is only one of five (Part 1) / five (Part 2) authors.

#### The Шыныбеков spelling question — settled
The record's Ә. / А. / А.Н. Шыныбеков are **one person**: the colophons print full names —
Шыныбеков Әбдухали Насырұлы = Абдухали Насырович Шыныбеков ("Ә.Н." in Kazakh, "А.Н." in
Russian). Co-author Д.Ә./Д.А. is his son Шыныбеков Даниар Әбдухалиұлы (Данияр Абдухалиевич).
General editor of the Шыныбеков books: М. Отелбаев (М. Otelbayev), academician.

### Work 1 — NIS *Mathematics*, Grade 7, Parts 1–2 (Nazarbayev Intellectual Schools, Astana, 2018)

**As printed.** Developed under the NIS Programme with the Institute of Education, University
College London. Part 1 authors: Н.В. Егоркина, Л.М. Забара, Н.Ю. Паникарская, И.И. Строева,
Ж.Ш. Шарипова. Part 2 authors: Д.А. Айтмухамет, Н.В. Егоркина, Л.М. Забара, Н.Ю. Паникарская,
И.И. Строева (Шарипова drops out, Айтмухамет joins). Publisher: АОО «Назарбаев Интеллектуальные
школы», Astana, 2018. Part 1: 144 pp; Part 2: 212 pp. ISBNs in the clustering table above.
Languages held: Kazakh, Russian, English — spot-checks (Unit 1.1–1.4 in KZ and EN; Part 2
Unit 1 in KZ and EN) show the same text, pagination and exercise sets in all three languages.
This is an **integrated** course: algebra, geometry and statistics alternate inside each part.
It is a workbook-style textbook: most of each page is tasks to do, with short theory boxes;
every unit ends with self-assessment pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout.

**Part 1** (printed pp.):
- Unit 1. Mathematical modelling of word problems (p.5): 1.1 Mathematical language —
  translating between words and symbols, numeric vs algebraic expressions, evaluating
  expressions (pp.6–7). 1.2 Solving linear equations (p.8) — root of an equation; the
  flow-chart for ax+b=0 covering the a=0 cases (single root / no root / infinitely many);
  equations with fractions cleared by a common denominator. 1.3 Solving linear inequalities
  (p.10) — properties, sign flip on multiplying/dividing by a negative, answers as intervals
  on the number line and in interval notation; double inequalities. 1.4 Solving systems of
  linear equations in two unknowns (p.12) — substitution and addition shown side by side on
  the same system (two pupils' board work). Word problems are modelled throughout
  (prices in tenge, distances Astana–Almaty, work rates).
- Unit 2. Powers with integer exponent ("Powers of numbers") (p.27).
- Unit 3. Linear equations and inequalities containing modulus / absolute value (p.61) — a
  topic the mainstream textbooks do **not** treat as its own unit.
- Unit 4. Basic concepts of geometry (p.71).
- Unit 5. Polynomials (p.101).
- Unit 6. Elements of statistics (p.129) — the part ends (p.144) with self-assessment on
  frequency tables (absolute/relative frequency), stem-and-leaf diagrams, mean/median/mode,
  cumulative frequency and frequency polygons.

**Part 2** (printed pp.):
- Unit 1. Triangles (p.5 in RU/EN; the KZ contents page prints 4 — a one-page contents
  discrepancy between language editions): 1.1 Triangle and its types — drawn/discovered
  definitions, classification by sides and by angles, median/bisector/altitude promised;
  congruence of triangles and proving congruence are the unit's stated outcomes.
- Unit 2. Formulas of abridged (short) multiplication (p.37).
- Unit 3. Parallel lines (p.73).
- Unit 4. Function and graph of function (p.113).
- Unit 5. Circle. Geometric constructions (p.143).
- Unit 6. Algebraic fractions (p.181).

**Methods/examples/problems.** Discovery format: short prompt questions ("Explain the
solution. If mistakes were made, correct them"), worked solutions presented as pupils' board
work to critique, matching/table tasks, "Remember!" boxes for definitions. Problems are
heavily contextual (Kazakhstan geography, tenge prices, school data) and include
self-assessment with mean/median/mode from real-looking data sets.
**Honest negatives:** no separate treatment of standard form/scientific notation as a unit
(it sits inside the powers material), no frequency-polygon-free statistics — statistics here
is richer than the mainstream books (stem-and-leaf, cumulative frequency).
**Slip spotted:** the Unit 1 opener map gives the Astana–Almaty distance as **951 km in the
Kazakh edition (p.5) but 971 km in the English edition (p.5)** — the language editions are
not perfectly synchronised.
**Defects:** none in the files sampled; all six files complete (Part 1 EN ends at printed
p.144; Part 2 EN ends with its colophon).

### Work 2 — Абылкасымова А.Е., Кучер Т.П., Корчевский В.Е., Жумагулова З.А., *Алгебра 7* (Мектеп, Almaty, 2017)

**As printed.** Textbook for Grade 7 of general-education schools, approved by the Ministry
of Education and Science of RK. © authors 2017. Kazakh edition: 272 pp, ISBN
978-601-07-0851-8. Russian edition: 288 pp, ISBN 978-601-07-0853-2 (the Russian text is set
looser — same §§; pagination is identical to the Kazakh through Chapter 2, then diverges
from Chapter 3 on, running ~7–19 pp later by Chapter 6/answers). Uzbek
edition: 288 pp, ISBN 978-601-07-0934-8, translator М. Йўлдош қизи. Uyghur edition
(Cyrillic-script Uyghur): imprint states 272 pp, ISBN 978-601-07-0919-5, translator
К. Мәнсүров. Content filed once from the complete Russian file (265); KZ/UZ/UG contents
pages match §-for-§. Page numbers below are Kazakh/Russian where they differ.
Structure: Introduction (p.6); revision exercises for the Grades 5–6 course (p.8); then 41
numbered paragraphs in 6 chapters, each chapter closing with a "Проверь себя!" (Test
yourself) set; final revision exercises (KZ 251 / RU 270) and answers (KZ 262 / RU 281).
Exercises are graded A (compulsory for all), B (medium), C (advanced), with icons for
success criteria, analysis questions, consolidation questions, independent-study tasks and
end-of-proof markers.

- **Ch. 1. Power with integer exponent** (p.28 in both KZ and RU): §1 power with natural exponent;
  §2 multiplying powers with the same base; §3 dividing powers with the same base, power with
  zero exponent; §4 raising a power to a power; §5 raising a product and a quotient to a
  power; §6 power with integer (negative) exponent; §7 properties of integer-exponent powers;
  §8 standard form of a number, word problems on very large/small quantities; §9 transforming
  expressions containing powers; number sequences containing powers.
- **Ch. 2. Polynomials** (§§10–17): §10 monomial, standard form of a monomial; §11 polynomial,
  its standard form and degree; §12 adding/subtracting polynomials; §13 multiplying
  polynomials; §14 dividing a monomial and a polynomial by a monomial; §15 factorising by
  taking out the common factor; §16 factorising by grouping; §17 identity transformations of
  expressions.
- **Ch. 3. Function. Graph of a function** (§§18–27): §18 function; §19 giving a function by
  a formula; §20 tabular method; §21 graphical method; §22 linear function and its graph;
  §23 mutual position of graphs of linear functions; §24 solving a system of two linear
  equations graphically; §25 y = ax² — properties and graph; §26 y = ax³; §27 y = k/x (k≠0).
- **Ch. 4. Elements of statistics** (§§28–30): §28 variation series; §29 absolute and relative
  frequency, frequency table; §30 frequency polygon. (No mean/median/mode chapter — averages
  are assumed from earlier grades; no stem-and-leaf, no cumulative frequency — contrast NIS.)
- **Ch. 5. Formulas of abridged multiplication** (§§31–36): §31 difference of squares;
  §32 square of a sum and of a difference; §33 cube of a sum and of a difference; §34 sum and
  difference of cubes; §35 identity transformations using the formulas; §36 solving word
  problems by setting up equations and inequalities.
- **Ch. 6. Algebraic fractions** (§§37–41): §37 the algebraic fraction; §38 its basic property
  (cancellation); §39 adding/subtracting; §40 multiplying, raising to a power, dividing;
  §41 identity transformations of algebraic expressions.

**Methods.** Each § opens with a "problem that will be solved as you master the new
knowledge", states definitions/properties in tinted boxes, proves properties, then graded
exercises. Worked examples are formal worked solutions in the text (not pupil work).
**Honest negatives:** linear equations/inequalities and systems are **not** systematically
taught here — they are revision material from Grades 5–6 (the course assumes them); no
modulus equations (contrast NIS Unit 3); no functions beyond y=ax², y=ax³, y=k/x; statistics
is only three paragraphs.
**Defects:** KZ file 239 ends at printed p.261 — the **Answers section (pp.262–272) is
missing**. UZ file 1449 ends at printed p.277 — answers (from p.279) missing. UG file 1373
ends at printed p.205 in the middle of §35 exercises — **everything from §36 to the end
(printed pp.206–272) is missing**, including all of Chapter 6. RU file 265 is complete.

### Work 3 — Абылкасымова et al., *Алгебра 7*, 2nd edition, revised and supplemented (Мектеп, 2025)

**As printed.** Same four authors. Cover: «Өңделіп, толықтырылған екінші басылымы» / «Второе
издание, переработанное, дополненное», approved by the Ministry of Education of RK. 280 pp
both languages. KZ ISBN 978-601-07-1741-1; RU ISBN 978-601-07-1742-8. © text 2017, design 2025.
Filed once; KZ and RU contents match §-for-§ (RU pagination +0–2 pp vs KZ).

Same 6 chapters and same 41 §§ as Work 2, in the same order, with these changes:
- pagination grows slightly in early chapters (Ch. 1 now starts at p.22 in both languages —
  in 2017 §1 stood at p.28; and §§8 and 9 swap topics: §8 becomes "Transforming expressions
  containing powers" (the 2017 §9 topic, without its sequences material) and §9 becomes
  «Санның стандарт түрі. Мәтінді есептерді шығару» / "Standard form of a number. Solving
  word problems" (the 2017 §8 topic));
- §§25–26 are now stated with the condition a ≠ 0: y = ax² (a≠0), y = ax³ (a≠0); §27 y = k/x;
- a **Subject index** (Пәндік көрсеткіштер / Предметный указатель, p.270) is added before the
  Answers (p.272) — new in this edition;
- the icon set adds video-material, functional-literacy, cognitive and research task markers.
No new chapters and no removed chapters against 2017: this is a refresh, not a rewrite.
The inside back cover of both files prints the seven abridged-multiplication formulas as a
reference sheet. Files complete (KZ 284 PDF pp, RU 282 PDF pp including covers).


### Work 4 — Шыныбеков Ә.Н., Шыныбеков Д.Ә., *Алгебра 7* (Атамура, Almaty, 2017)

**As printed.** Textbook for Grade 7, general editor М. Отелбаев. KZ: 200 pp, ISBN
978-601-306-748-3. RU: 208 pp, ISBN 978-601-306-747-6. Contents are printed at the END of the
book (RU pp.204–206). Exercises carry double numbers (chapter.exercise) and are grouped
A (basic), B (medium), C (advanced), * (high difficulty/creative). Filed from the RU file;
the KZ file's opening revision (§0, exercises 0.1–0.11) matches the RU page-for-page in
content. Printed page = PDF page.

- Revision of Grades 5–6 material (p.4) — 27 oral questions, then exercise set 0.
- **Ch. 1. Power with natural and integer exponents** (p.11): 1.1 power with natural exponent
  (concept; multiplying/dividing powers with natural exponent; zero exponent, p.17; raising a
  power/product/quotient to a power, p.23). 1.2 power with integer exponent (concept of a
  negative-integer exponent, p.29; properties, p.30). 1.3 applications (p.34): sequences whose
  terms contain powers; standard form of a number (p.35); sums/differences in standard form
  (p.41);
  **absolute and relative error** (p.42) — an approximation topic the Mektep book treats only
  inside §8 word problems.
- **Ch. 2. Monomials and polynomials** (p.48): 2.1 monomials (standard form; multiplying
  monomials; raising a monomial to a power). 2.2 polynomials (standard form; adding and
  subtracting). 2.3 product of a monomial and a polynomial (incl. taking out the common
  factor, p.59). 2.4 product of polynomials (incl. factorising by grouping, p.67).
- **Ch. 3. Function** (p.75): 3.1 function and ways of giving it (concept; value table and
  graph, p.80). 3.2 linear function and its graph (p.86): direct proportionality y = kx —
  introduced through priced-goods and motion contexts (flour at 260 tg/kg), graph plotted from
  a value table, k named the angular coefficient; y = kx + b (p.88); mutual position of lines
  (p.94); position relative to the coordinate axes (p.95). 3.3 solving systems of linear
  equations graphically (graph of a linear equation in two variables, p.99; graphical method,
  p.100). 3.4 graphs of y = x² and y = x³ and their properties (p.106); y = ax², y = ax³
  (p.110). 3.5 function y = k/x and its graph (p.115).
- **Ch. 4. Elements of statistics** (p.121): 4.1 population and sample; 4.2 frequency polygon
  of absolute and relative frequencies (p.126).
- **Ch. 5. Formulas of abridged multiplication** (p.134): 5.1 square of a sum / of a difference.
  5.2 difference of squares (p.140) and factorising by it. 5.3 sum and difference of cubes and
  factorising by them (p.146). 5.4 cube of a sum / of a difference (p.150). 5.5 transforming
  whole expressions; methods of factorising a polynomial (p.154–155). 5.6 solving word
  problems by building their mathematical models (p.159).
- **Ch. 6. Rational expressions** (p.166): 6.1 concept; reducing rational fractions. 6.2 sum
  and difference of fractions with the same denominator (p.172) and with different
  denominators (p.173). 6.3 multiplying (p.179) and dividing (p.180) fractions; identity
  transformations of rational expressions (p.184).
- **Ch. 7. Revision exercises** for the Grade 7 course; Answers from p.195.

**Methods.** Dense, university-lecture style compressed for school: definition in a tinted
box, bulleted restatements, a small number of fully worked "Пример" examples, then very large
graded exercise sets. Worked Example (1.1.1, p.12) evaluates −3⁴ + (−2)⁶ term by term before
combining. Word problems are fewer and harder than in the Mektep book; group C is aimed at
maths-depth classes (the preface says so explicitly).
**Honest negatives:** no modulus equations; statistics is the thinnest of the three algebra
lines (population/sample + frequency polygon only); no separate "linear equations and
inequalities" chapter — assumed known.
**Slip checked and not found:** an earlier reading of this draft reported a misprint in the
worked example on printed p.12 ("(−2)⁵ = 2⁶ = 64"). A 300-dpi re-render of p.12 shows the
step printed correctly as (−2)⁶ = 2⁶ = 64; the example is sound (see the citation-check
block at the end of this file).
**Defects:** none found; both files complete (colophons present).

### Work 5 — Шыныбеков Ә.Н., Шыныбеков Д.Ә. + Бектасова А.И. (KZ) / Амалбекова Л.Е. (RU), *Алгебра 7* (Атамура, 2025)

**As printed.** 224 pp both languages. KZ ISBN 978-601-10-0651-4; RU ISBN 978-601-10-0650-7.
Colophons give the authors' full names: Шыныбеков Абдухали Насырович, Шыныбеков Данияр
Абдухалиевич; Бектасова Алмагүл Ибраимқызы (KZ) / Амалбекова Лунара Еркиновна (RU).
Contents at the end (RU pp.221–223). Same 7 chapters, same section skeleton and largely the
same exercise stock as Work 4 (the opening revision exercises drop the 2017 "0." numbering —
they run as plain numbers — and are lightly re-edited, e.g. two 2017 items merged into one),
with these
2025 additions: a **test after every chapter** ("Тесты к разделу…"); a final test delivered by
**QR code** (p.200) with solutions/answers to selected exercises also by QR (p.201);
functional-literacy tasks (p.202); **term names in three languages** (p.205); a glossary
(p.208); answers from p.212. Section 3.1.2 is re-titled "Giving a function by table and
graph" and 4.2 becomes "Polygons of absolute and relative frequencies". Filed as a distinct
work (new edition, new apparatus), content not duplicated in the filing.
**Defects:** none found; files complete.

### Work 6 — Yelemessov A. et al., *Algebra, Grade 7: Textbook*, 1st edition (Astana-Kitap, Almaty, 2022) — ENGLISH

**As printed.** Authors: Akzhol Yelemessov, Azat Turapbekov, Smail Abibulla, Rauan
Zhanarbekuly, Yersultan Batyrbekov, Aslan Nurbayev. Recommended by the Ministry of Education
of RK. 288 pp, ISBN 978-601-7595-88-3. English throughout; every page carries a Terminology
box giving each key term in Kazakh and Russian. Structure per chapter: sections with
"Practice" sets after every worked example, end-of-section Exercises graded A (easy) to C
(difficult), then Summative Test, Real-Life Application and Summary; final Review Exercises
for Grade 7 (p.269), Answer Key (p.276), Glossary (p.284). Review Exercises for Grades 5–6
open the book (p.8). Printed page = PDF page.

- **Ch. 1. Integer exponents** (p.18): natural exponents and their properties in three
  sections (multiplication/division/power-to-a-power; product and quotient powers);
  applications — last digit of a power, how area/volume scale; integer exponents and their
  properties (p.36); simplifying exponential expressions, incl. sequences with repeated
  multiplication (p.47); scientific notation (p.50), operations in it (p.54) and using it
  (comparing numbers, unit conversions, p.58); approximation (p.62) and approximation errors
  — absolute and relative error (p.66); applying scientific notation to word problems (p.72).
- **Ch. 2. Polynomials** (p.86): monomials — degree, coefficient (worked table method);
  standard form and factorising a monomial, GCF of monomials; polynomials — degree, standard
  form; adding/subtracting; multiplying (monomial×polynomial, polynomial×polynomial);
  factorising by taking out the GCF (incl. negative and binomial factors); factorising by
  grouping; two further "factoring polynomials" sections (pp.114, 118).
- **Ch. 3. Functions** (p.130): introduction to function; **domain and range** (p.136) —
  treated explicitly, unlike the KZ/RU books; y = mx; y = mx + n; the **intercept method**
  (p.148); relative positions of graphs of linear functions; solving systems of linear
  equations by graphing (p.158); y = ax² (p.162); y = ax³ (p.168); y = k/x (p.172).
- **Ch. 4. Elements of statistics** (p.186): variation series — population and sample;
  absolute and relative frequency; presenting/interpreting data — **bar graph, line graph,
  pie chart** (pp.194–196), which neither KZ/RU algebra book teaches.
- **Ch. 5. Basic identities** (p.208): difference of two squares; square of a binomial; cube
  of a binomial; sum and difference of cubes; identities in calculation; identities in
  problem solving.
- **Ch. 6. Rational expressions** (p.242): rational expression and its domain; simplifying
  (incl. ratios of −1); multiplication and division; addition/subtraction with like and
  unlike denominators (LCD); working with rational expressions.

**Methods.** Conversational, fully explained worked examples ("Example — Solution — Practice"
triplets on almost every spread, e.g. §2.1 monomials p.86 and §5.1 difference of squares
p.208), discussion prompts, pair activities, "spot the error" tasks, Research/Fact boxes.
The difference-of-squares rule is first motivated by factorising x²−4 with number pairs
before the formula is stated.
**Error spotted in the book:** §5.1, Example 3 (printed p.209): "Factorize completely:
6a² − 130" is worked as 6a² − 130 = 6·(a² − 25) = 6(a+5)(a−5). That is wrong — 6·25 = 150,
not 130; 6a²−130 does not factorise this way. (Examples 1, 2 and 4 on the same pages are
correct.)
**Honest negatives:** no equations/inequalities chapter beyond what graphing systems needs;
no modulus; factorising stops at GCF/grouping/identities (no trinomial factorising).
**Defects:** none; file complete.

### Work 7 — Кучер Т.П., Жумагулова З.А., Корчевский В.Е., *Алгебра 7: Дидактические материалы* (Мектеп, 2017) — Russian. NOT a textbook

**As printed.** "Учебное пособие" (study aid) for Grade 7, 52 pp, ISBN 978-601-07-0889-1.
The introduction states plainly that it is a **supplement to Work 2** (the Абылкасымова
textbook). It teaches nothing: it contains independent works (самостоятельные работы) in
4 variants each (variants marked * are harder), organised by the textbook's topics —
opening set: powers with natural exponent and their properties (p.4); sampled sets cover
polynomials and operations on them (p.10), statistics — frequency tables and polygons from
real data such as shoe-production and 100 m race results (p.20), algebraic fractions
(p.30), functions and graphs incl. solving x² = 1/x graphically (p.40) — and **control
works** (контрольные работы), the final Grade 7 control work in 4 variants designed for
2 hours (p.40 shows Контрольная работа № 4 in progress). Answers to the control works are
at the end. Filed as didactic/assessment materials, a companion to Work 2.

### Work 8 — Жумагулова З.А., Жумалиева Л.Д., *Алгебра 7: Сборник задач* (Мектеп, 2017) — Russian. NOT a textbook

**As printed.** Problem collection for Grade 7, 36 pp, ISBN 978-601-07-0890-7. **The record's
author "Кучер Т.П." is wrong**: cover and imprint give З.А. Жумагулова and Л.Д. Жумалиева only.
The introduction states it is compiled to match Work 2's textbook. Problems are numbered by
the textbook's chapters: Ch. 1 integer exponents (from p.4: evaluating powers, writing
repeated products as powers, cube-face/volume problems); Ch. 2 polynomials (p.12 sampled:
grouping factorisation, solving equations and inequalities built on polynomial products,
proving identities); Ch. 3 functions; Ch. 4 statistics (p.20: building variation series from
pupils' own data — product prices, classmates' birth months, marks; absolute/relative frequency
of a variant; frequency polygons from Almaty temperature data); Ch. 5 abridged multiplication;
Ch. 6 algebraic fractions and operations on them (p.28: multi-step combined operations,
equations with fractional coefficients, solving an equation for x in terms of parameters).
Answers occupy the final pages (p.34 sampled: answers for Chs. 3–6). No theory is taught.
Filed as a problem collection, companion to Work 2.

### Work 9 — Смирнов В.А., Туяков Е.А., *Геометрия 7* (Мектеп, 2017)

**As printed.** Textbook for Grade 7. Colophon names: Смирнов Владимир Алексеевич, Туяков
Есенгельды Алыбаевич. 144 pp in every language edition. KZ ISBN 978-601-07-0872-3; RU ISBN
978-601-07-0873-0; UZ ISBN 978-601-07-0932-4 (translator М. Йўлдош қизи). Contents at the end
(RU p.143). Exercises graded A (basic/mandatory), B (medium), C (advanced); a test
("Проверь себя!") closes each chapter; a glossary (p.138) and answers (p.141) close the book.
Filed from the RU file (266); the KZ file (240) is the same book scanned as narrow
single-page strips (216×360 pt pages, one printed page per PDF page, some cropped); UZ
(1433) matches chapter-for-chapter.

- **Ch. 1. Basic geometric concepts** (p.5): §1 basic notions — point, line, plane as
  idealisations; Euclid and the axiomatic idea explained through a chess-elephant analogy
  (axioms as "rules of the game"); the axiom that through any two points passes a unique line;
  parallel lines defined. §2 rays and segments (p.10). §3 measuring segment lengths (p.15).
  §4 half-plane and angle (p.19). §5 operations on angles, equality of angles (p.23). §6
  measuring angles (p.31). Test p.37.
- **Ch. 2. Triangles** (p.39): §7 the triangle and its types (definition; acute/right/obtuse;
  median, bisector, altitude, midline; perimeter; congruent triangles defined by matching
  sides/angles). §8 first congruence criterion (SAS). §9 second criterion (ASA). §10 isosceles
  triangles. §11 third criterion (SSS). §12 relations between sides and angles of a triangle.
  §13 right triangles. Test p.77.
- **Ch. 3. Mutual position of lines** (p.79): §14 perpendicular and slant (oblique). §15
  parallel lines. §16 sum of the angles of a triangle. §17 triangle inequality. Test p.98.
- **Ch. 4. Circle. Geometric constructions** (p.101): §18 circle and disc. §19 mutual position
  of a line and a circle. §20 mutual position of two circles. §21 loci of points (геометрические
  места точек). §22 circumscribed and inscribed circles of a triangle. §23 construction
  problems. Test p.134.

**Methods.** Narrative theory with full proofs, historical/logical framing (the axiomatic
method is itself a teaching topic), then A/B/C problems mixing computation, drawing on grid
paper, and proof; e.g. §7 asks pupils to construct triangles on squared paper and count
medians/bisectors/altitudes, and to prove statements about a pentagram figure.
**Honest negatives:** no coordinates, no vectors, no area of a triangle, no similarity — all
later-grade topics; constructions are limited to the basic compass-and-ruler set in §23.
**Defects:** the **Uyghur file 1424 is a fragment** — printed pp.39–141 only (PDF pp.1–103);
cover, title, imprint, contents, all of Chapter 1 and the start of Chapter 2 (§§1–6 and the
opening of §7 fall in the missing pp.1–38), and the last answers pages (142–144) are missing.
Its identity was proved from §/exercise numbering and chapter titles matching the other
editions. KZ/RU/UZ files are complete.

### Work 10 — Смирнов В.А., Туяков Е.А., Мамутова Д.С., Сансызбай Т.К., *Геометрия 7*, 2nd edition (Мектеп, 2025)

**As printed.** «Өңделіп, толықтырылған екінші басылымы» / «Второе издание, переработанное,
дополненное». 152 pp. KZ ISBN 978-601-07-1749-7; RU ISBN 978-601-07-1750-3. Full names from
the colophon: Мамутова Дария Сапаковна, Сансызбай Толганай Канапиякызы. The 2025 book keeps
Work 9's four chapters and its text — §1 (printed p.5) is the 2017 text re-set, including the
chess-elephant axiom passage — and adds lesson apparatus: a "Today in the lesson" outcome box
and "Key words" box on every § opening, QR codes, and functional-literacy / video /
research task icons; pagination grows 144 → 152 pp. A full contents is printed at the end
(p.150, listing the glossary at p.143, bibliography p.146 and answers p.147); p.3 is only a
chapter-title divider page and p.4 the introduction. Filed as a distinct edition; maths
content as Work 9.
**Defects:** none found; files complete.

### Work 11 — Шыныбеков Ә.Н., Шыныбеков Д.Ә., *Геометрия 7* (Атамура, 2017)

**As printed.** KZ: 96 pp, ISBN 978-601-306-750-6. RU: 80 pp, ISBN 978-601-306-749-0 (the two
language editions genuinely differ in extent — denser setting in Russian). General editor
М. Отелбаев. A long introduction (RU pp.5–6) teaches the axiomatic method itself — why
definitions cannot regress forever (the ⇒-chain and cycle diagrams are printed), axioms,
theorems, proof — before any figure is studied. Contents at the end (RU p.79). Filed from RU.

- **Ch. 1. Initial concepts and axioms of planimetry** (p.7): 1.1 point, line, segment. 1.2
  half-plane, ray, angle; laying off segments and angles (p.16); axioms and theorems (p.17).
  1.3 triangles (p.21); existence of a triangle equal to a given one (p.22); parallel lines
  introduced (p.22). 1.4 adjacent and vertical angles (p.25); perpendicular lines (p.26);
  angle bisector (p.27); **method of proof by contradiction** (p.27).
- **Ch. 2. Criteria for the congruence of triangles and their consequences** (p.31): 2.1 first
  (SAS) and second (ASA) criteria — both **proved by superposition** on the "existence of an
  equal triangle" axiom (worked proof pp.31–32, with a worked example finding BD from
  intersecting segments); altitude, bisector, median and midline of a triangle (p.35);
  properties of an isosceles triangle and the converse theorem (p.36); third (SSS) criterion
  (p.38).
- **Ch. 3. Mutual position of lines** (p.42): 3.1 criteria for parallelism of lines; angles
  formed by a transversal (p.42); sum of the angles of a triangle (p.44); right triangle
  (p.44). 3.2 relations between sides and angles of a triangle (p.51); triangle inequality
  (p.52).
- **Ch. 4. Circle and geometric constructions** (p.55): 4.1 circle; circumscribed circle
  (p.56) — tangent defined, tangent ⊥ radius proved, diameter-through-midpoint-of-chord
  theorem (p.55–56); inscribed circle (p.58). 4.2 geometric constructions (p.62): simplest
  constructions; constructing a triangle from three elements (p.64); construction problems
  (p.65).
- **Ch. 5. Revision exercises** (p.70). Before the answers the book prints two visual summary
  spreads (pp.73–74): types of triangles, medians/bisectors/altitudes, angle-sum and triangle
  inequality, circumscribed/inscribed circles, the three parallelism criteria, and the
  simplest constructions (bisect a segment, bisect an angle, build a triangle by SSS and by
  SAS). Answers and hints from p.75 — the Ch. 5 answers include full reasoning, not just
  results.

**Methods.** The most proof-driven Grade 7 geometry in the band: every criterion is a named,
proved Theorem; worked examples follow proofs immediately; exercises are A/B/C with *
problems, plus "formulate and prove" questions and practical (ПЗ) tasks (e.g. draw a triangle
with protractor and ruler to given data).
**Honest negatives:** no loci chapter (contrast Smirnov §21), no separate inscribed/
circumscribed problem set beyond 4.1, no coordinates or area.
**Defects:** none found; both files complete.

### Work 12 — Шыныбеков et al., *Геометрия 7* (Атамура, 2025)

**As printed.** 128 pp both languages (the 2017 book's 80/96 pp grow to a uniform 128).
KZ ISBN 978-601-10-0657-6 (third author А.И. Бектасова); RU ISBN 978-601-10-0668-2 (third
author Л.Е. Амалбекова). Contents at the end (RU pp.126–127). Same five-chapter plan as
Work 11, re-sectioned: Ch. 1 now separates adjacent/vertical angles, perpendiculars and the
angle bisector into 1.3, and proof by contradiction into 1.4 (p.28); each chapter gains
"Additional exercises" and a **trilingual term list** ("Названия терминов на трех языках");
Ch. 2 merges the triangle material as 2.1 and the criteria as 2.2 (altitude/bisector/median/
midline at p.45, isosceles properties and converse at p.45, third criterion at p.48); Ch. 3 is
re-titled "Criteria for the parallelism of lines"; Ch. 4 adds tangent properties explicitly
(4.1.2, p.81) and circumscribed (p.82) / inscribed (p.84) circles; Ch. 5 revision now includes
**chapter tests** (p.106), a glossary (p.116) and answers (p.120). Filed as a distinct
edition. **Defects:** none found; files complete.

### Work 13 — Turapbekov A. et al., *Geometry, Grade 7: Textbook*, 1st edition (Astana-Kitap, Almaty, 2022) — ENGLISH

**As printed.** Authors: Azat Turapbekov, Ainur Kudaibergen, Akzhol Yelemessov, Zhassur
Batyrov, Amaner Akhmet, Dina Tengdik, Zhumadulla Abdulkahkov. 186 pp, ISBN
978-601-7595-87-6. English; Terminology boxes give terms in Kazakh and Russian. Same
Astana-Kitap apparatus as Work 6: Practice after each example, A/B/C problems, chapter
Summary, Review Exercises (p.176), Answer Key (p.180), Glossary (p.182). Printed page = PDF
page. The content tracks the Smirnov course closely but is re-authored, not translated.

- **Ch. 1. Introduction to geometry** (p.10): §1 basic notions, axioms, theorems — point,
  line (axioms of belonging, ordering, intersection), segment (distance and measuring axioms),
  ray, axioms of laying off segments, plane, half-plane, angle and its measurement,
  parallelness, triangle, the axiom of existence of a triangle equal to a given one, "axioms
  and theorems". §2 congruence of figures (p.28) — corresponding elements, congruent triangles.
  §3 **methods of proof of theorems** (p.36) — direct method and proof by contradiction, given
  their own section. §4 adjacent and vertical angles and their properties (p.40), incl.
  perpendicular. Summary p.50.
- **Ch. 2. Triangles** (p.54): §1 triangle and its types (by sides: scalene/isosceles/
  equilateral; by angles). §2 auxiliary elements — median, bisector, altitude (incl. where
  altitudes fall), perpendicular bisector, **triangle midline** (p.64). §3 congruent triangles
  (p.68) — SSS, SAS, ASA, AAS presented as "Axiom 1"-style criteria after a patty-paper
  cutting activity; worked examples use congruence to solve for unknowns. §4 isosceles
  triangle and its properties; equilateral properties. Summary p.86.
- **Ch. 3. Mutual positions of lines** (p.90): §1 parallel lines — angles formed by a
  transversal, mixed problems, proving lines parallel. §2 angle sum of a triangle; exterior
  angle theorem and exterior-angle sum theorem (p.112). §3 triangle inequality (p.122) with
  the longer-side/larger-angle relations. §4 properties of a right triangle (p.128) — right
  triangle congruence, properties. §5 perpendicular lines; perpendicular, oblique line and its
  projection (p.134). Summary p.138.
- **Ch. 4. Circle. Geometric construction** (p.142): §1 circle and its parts, central angle —
  **locus** defined (p.142), chords and their properties. §2 relative position of a line and a
  circle; of two circles (non-intersecting, tangent, intersecting). §3 tangent to a circle,
  secants, properties of tangents (p.156). §4 inscribed and circumscribed circles of a
  triangle (p.160). §5 construction problems (p.164) — compass use taught step by step;
  constructing an angle equal to a given angle (six-step worked construction on grid paper),
  bisecting an angle, dividing a segment in half, constructing a triangle from given elements.

**Methods.** Same discovery-plus-formal-proof blend as Work 6: hands-on activities (patty
paper congruence), "Research time" boxes (triangle rigidity and Baiterek), fully narrated
examples, trilingual terminology on every spread.
**Slip spotted:** §2.3 Example 2 (p.69) solves 17 = 3x − 1 and concludes "x = 6 cm" — the
value is right but x itself is a pure number in the equation; a minor unit slip.
**Honest negatives:** no coordinates/vectors/area; proof-by-contradiction appears only inside
Ch. 1 §3, not revisited.
**Defects:** none; file complete.

### Work 14 — Смирнов В.А., Туяков Е.А., *Геометрия 7: Дидактические материалы* (Мектеп, 2017) — Russian. NOT a textbook

**As printed.** Study aid for Grade 7, 56 pp, ISBN 978-601-07-0892-1; the preface states it
supplements Work 9. Contents: **mathematical dictations** (complete-the-sentence prompts,
e.g. on side–angle relations and right triangles, p.8), oral exercises (the preface argues
oral analysis is the core geometry skill; sampled: pairs of congruent triangles and proof
tasks, p.20; tangents and circle problems 4.37–4.47, p.32), independent works in 2 variants
per topic (from p.33; sampled: side-relations and circle independent works, p.44), and
4 control works in 2 variants (from p.48). Answers to independent and control works at the
end (p.51). No theory is taught.
**Scan defect:** several pages of this file are skewed/shifted so that line beginnings are
clipped at the left edge (clearly visible on the sampled p.20) — readable, but the copy is
the roughest scan in the band.

### Work 15 — Смирнов В.А., Туяков Е.А., *Геометрия 7: Сборник задач* (Мектеп, 2017) — Russian. NOT a textbook

**As printed.** Problem collection for Grade 7, 24 pp, ISBN 978-601-07-0893-8; compiled to
match Work 9 (introduction says so). Problems numbered by the textbook's four chapters:
Ch. 1 basic concepts (from p.4: points/lines incidence, segments, rays — e.g. how many parts
ten points divide a line into); Ch. 2 triangles (p.10 sampled: congruent-triangle proofs from
figures, perimeter problems, drawing altitudes/bisectors/medians of acute, right and obtuse
triangles); Ch. 3 mutual position of lines (p.16 sampled: exterior angles of a triangle in
many forms); Ch. 4 circle and constructions (problems pp.17–20). The back of the book (pp.21–22) gives
answers **and hints/full solution sketches** for harder problems (e.g. 3.6, 3.17, 4.31–4.40),
then the contents (p.23) and the colophon (p.24). No theory is taught. Filed as a problem collection, companion to
Work 9. **Defects:** none; file complete at its stated 24 pp.

---

### Band notes — comparisons a lesson-builder needs

1. **Three parallel Grade 7 systems coexist.** (i) The mainstream Mektep pair — Абылкасымова
   Algebra + Смирнов Geometry (2017, refreshed 2025) — with their four companion books.
   (ii) The Атамура pair — Шыныбеков Algebra + Geometry (2017, refreshed 2025) — denser and
   more proof-heavy, with statistics reduced and an explicit axiomatic-method introduction.
   (iii) The NIS integrated *Mathematics* (2018, KZ/RU/EN), the only course that mixes algebra,
   geometry and statistics in one book and the only one teaching modulus equations and richer
   statistics (stem-and-leaf, cumulative frequency). The Astana-Kitap English pair (2022)
   follows the mainstream syllabus but re-authors it with domain/range, pie charts and
   explicit proof methods.
2. **Common core across all lines:** integer exponents and standard form; monomials/
   polynomials and factorising (common factor, grouping); abridged-multiplication identities
   (a²−b², (a±b)², (a±b)³, a³±b³); functions y = kx, y = kx + b, graphical solution of systems,
   y = ax², y = ax³, y = k/x; algebraic/rational fractions; statistics limited to variation
   series, frequencies and frequency polygons; geometry = basic concepts/axioms, triangles
   and the three congruence criteria, isosceles and right triangles, parallel lines and the
   angle sum, triangle inequality, circle basics, inscribed/circumscribed circles, elementary
   constructions.
3. **What Grade 7 Kazakhstan does NOT teach** (all lines): quadratic equations, factorising
   trinomials, square roots, similarity, trigonometry, coordinates/vectors, triangle area.
   Linear equations/inequalities and systems are *assumed* from Grades 5–6 except in the NIS
   book, which re-teaches them in Unit 1.

stated printed page, worked examples, ISBNs, authors, publisher/year, page counts, print
runs, stated defects), each verified by rendering the exact PDF page. All 15 works covered;
no work left unchecked.

Signature claims verified as printed in the books: Work 1's Astana–Almaty map slip (971 km
EN p.5 vs 951 km KZ p.5); Work 6 §5.1 Example 3 (p.209) really does factorise 6a²−130 as
6(a²−25); Work 11's superposition proofs (pp.31–32, Axiom VIII); Work 13 §3 Example 2
(p.69) really does conclude "x = 6 cm", and §4.1 (p.142) opens with the locus definition.

1. **Work 4, "slip" on p.12:** the reported misprint "(−2)⁵ = 2⁶ = 64" does not exist — a
   300-dpi render of printed p.12 (file 263) shows the step printed correctly as
   (−2)⁶ = 2⁶ = 64 and the result −17 is right. The "Slip spotted" paragraph was replaced
   with a checked-and-not-found note.
2. **Work 5, "opening revision set 0.1–0.12 is identical" to 2017:** no such set exists in
   the 2025 book — the revision exercises are renumbered without the "0." prefix and
   lightly re-edited (2017's 0.25 + 0.26 appear merged as 2025's item 25). Evidence: file
   1512 printed p.9 against file 263 printed p.9. The sentence was rewritten accordingly.

Method note: `pdfinfo` page counts are unreliable for these PDFs (inflated/deflated root
/Count); all extents above were confirmed with PyMuPDF page counts and pdftoppm behaviour.
All renders used for this check were deleted after use.

## Kazakhstan Maths — Band E-G8: Grade 8 Algebra + Geometry

Band: 24 files, 4,308 PDF pages (as recorded). Every file's cover, title page and
imprint were inspected (pdftotext where a text layer exists; rendered pages
everywhere else). Recorded titles in set-table.json proved unreliable for this
band: several files are in languages the record never mentions (Uyghur, Uzbek),
one "student textbook" is a teacher aid, and the four "Рыскельдиева Algebra"
files are a combined NIS *Mathematics* course. Covers/imprints govern below.

### Clustering rule applied

(a) Same edition + language + part in two files = one work in two copies
    (verified by identical imprint, identical TOC, and identical interior pages).
(b) Same series + part in different languages = ONE work, filed once, all
    language editions listed; contents spot-checked across languages
    (exercise sets and section sequences compared — in every case here the
    language editions carry the same sections and the same numbered exercises,
    with language-edition pagination differing by a few pages).
(c) Different publisher/year/ISBN = distinct work.
(d) Author-name spellings follow each book's own imprint: the Mektep algebra
    author is **Әбілқасымова** in the Kazakh/Uyghur/Uzbek imprints and
    **Абылкасымова** in the Russian imprint — one person (full name in the
    Russian colophon: Абылкасымова Алма Есимбековна). The Astana-kitap English
    books print **Yelemessov** (Akzhol Yelemessov) on the title page and in the
    bibliographic entry ("A. Yelemessov"); the back cover of the Algebra book
    misprints the first name as "Akhol". The record's "Yelemesov/Yelemessov"
    variation is therefore settled: **Yelemessov** is the imprint spelling.

### Clustering table — every file → its work

#### Work 1 — NIS *Mathematics*, Grade 8 (combined algebra+geometry course), NIS-Programme, Nur-Sultan, 2019
Authors (imprint): Г.К. Апеева, Г.Д. Жампеисова, Г.Б. Кожахметова, Г.К. Рыскельдиева
(Part 2 adds Н.В. Егоркина). Publisher: АОО «Назарбаев Интеллектуальные школы» (NIS), 2019.
| File | What it actually is |
|---|---|
| algebra_student_1077 (192 PDF pp) | Part 1, **Kazakh**, ISBN 978-601-328-329-6 (part 1); 978-601-328-330-2 (set) |
| algebra_student_1078 (192 PDF pp) | Part 1, Kazakh — second copy of the same scan (file size differs by 2 bytes; imprint, TOC and interior pages identical) |
| algebra_student_1079 (256 PDF pp) | Part 2, **Kazakh**, ISBN 978-601-328-540-5 (part 2); 978-601-328-330-2 (set) |
